nakai		has
aḻu		rut
kol		haṉ
(4) mēlum, vaṭamoḻiyiliruntu tōṉṟiyaṉavē tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ eṉṟu nampiya mēṉāṭṭārāycci yāḷarkaḷukku, vaṭa coṟkaḷaiyē eṭuttāḷāmalō aṅkoṉṟum iṅkoṉṟumāka mikavum arukiya nilaiyilmaṭṭum eṭuttāṇṭō varum tiruntā moḻikaḷum tirāviṭa moḻi yiṉattil irukkiṉṟaṉa eṉpatē teriyātu. aṉṟiyum, vaṭacohṟkaḷai ōraḷaviṟku eṭuttāḷum tiruntiya tirāviṭa moḻikaḷum kaṭṭāyamāka avaṟṟai eṭuttāḷattāṉ vēṇṭumeṉpatillāmal, viruppamuḷatēṟ payaṉpaṭuttiyum, iṉṟēl aṟavē yotukkiyum varukiṉṟaṉa eṉpatum avarkaḷukkut teriyātu. āṉāl, iṉṟaiya nilaiyai nōkkiṉ, vaṭamoḻiyai aṟavē nīkkuvateṉpatu teluṅku moḻikku aritākum; kaṉṉaṭa moḻikkō mikavum aritu; malaiyāḷa moḻikkō aritiṉumaritu. im moḻikaḷ kaṇakku vaḻakkillāmal vaṭa coṟkaḷai eṭuttāṇṭu vantuḷḷamai yālum, ac coṟkaḷiṉ utaviyai nāṭuvatē vaḻakkamākak koṇṭuḷḷamaiyālum, tattam ciṟappup paṇpukaḷai yiḻantu, taṉittu niṉṟiyaṅkum āṟṟalaiyum iḻantu niṟkiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl, tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ aṉaittiṉum mikavum tiruntiya paṇpaṭṭa nilaiyiluḷḷa tamiḻmoḻiyō, vēṇṭumeṉṟāl, vaṭacoṟkaḷai aṟavē akaṟṟit taṉittiyaṅkum āṟṟal vāyntiruppa tōṭaṉṟi, avaṟṟiṉ utavi yillāmal mikavum mēmpaṭṭu, vaḷamuṟṟu miḷirum āṟṟalum vāyntatākum.
paṇṭait tūya tamiḻmoḻikkuc centamiḻ eṉṟu peyar. ic centamiḻilēyē tamiḻmoḻiyiṟ kāṇappaṭum paṇṭaiya ilakkiyaṅkaḷiṟ perumpālaṉa iyaṉṟuḷḷaṉa. āṉāl, avaṟṟuḷ kāṇappaṭum vaṭacoṟkaḷō mikamikak kuṟaivu. iṉṟaiya pēccut tamiḻuṭaṉum, urainaṭait tamiḻuṭaṉum innilai māṟupaṭṭatoṉṟākak kāṇappaṭum. etaṉāl avvāṟeṉilō, kaṇṇuṅ karuttumāy iruntu vaṭacoṟ kaḷaiyum, vaṭamoḻi yeḻuttukkaḷaiyum vilakki, tūya tamiḻccoṟkaḷ, marapumoḻikaḷ, ākiyavaṟṟaiyē kaiyāṇṭu vantuḷḷamaiyiṉālētāṉ. tamiḻmakkaḷiṭaiyē ic centamiḻ moḻip pātukāppu evvaḷavukkup paraviyirunta teṉpatu, eḻutappaṭṭa oru tamiḻnūlil evvaḷavuk kevvaḷavu vaṭa coṟkaḷ arukik kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉavō, avvaḷavukku avvaḷavu atu ciṟantatoru nūleṉṟum, evvaḷavuk kevvaḷavu vaṭacoṟkaḷ mikutiyākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉavō avvaḷavukkavvaḷavu atu tāḻntatoru nūleṉṟum karuti matippiṭum paḻakkam paṇṭutoṭṭup payiṉṟu varukiṉṟamai yiṉālēyē iṉitu teḷiyappaṭum. piṟamoḻi nūlkaḷ cilavaṟṟil evvaḷavuk kevvaḷavu vaṭamoḻic coṟkaḷ payiṉṟu varukiṉṟaṉavō avvaḷavukkavvaḷavu annūlkaḷ avvammoḻiyiṉarāṟ ciṟappuṭaṉ pōṟṟappaṭum; tamiḻilō evvaḷavukkevvaḷavu tamiḻ nūlkaḷ vaṭamoḻiyiṉ utaviyai nāṭāmal taṉittiyaṅkukiṉṟaṉavō avvaḷavuk kavvaḷavu  ciṟappuṭaṉ pōṟṟappaṭum. uṇṇāṭṭuc ciṟṟūrkaḷilum, nāṭṭuppuṟaṅkaḷilum vāḻntuvarum tāḻnta makkaḷiṭaiyē vaṭamoḻic coṟkaḷaip pēccuvaḻakkilum kaiyāḷāmal otukkum tūya paḻakkam kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. oru moḻiyiṉ toṉmaic ciṟappunilai am moḻiyiyalilum ceyyuḷ kaḷilum, tāḻnta kuṭimakkaḷiṉ pēccukaḷilumiruntē ārāyntu kāṇappaṭum eṉpatu orupotu uṇmaiyākum. piṟkālattil mikavum valintu muyaṉṟu eḻutappaṭṭa tamiḻ urainaṭai nūlkaḷilum, pārppaṉarkaḷ pēcum tamiḻilum, mikavum kaṟṟavar kaḷākak karutappaṭum tamiḻarkaḷiṉ pēccilumē vaṭamoḻi mikaipaṭap paravi vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. atuvum, camaya uṇmai, aṟiviyal, tattuvam, ēṉaik kalaikaḷiluḷḷa marapuc coṟkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟai viḷakkumiṭaṅkaḷil maṭṭumē ivvāṟu vaṭacoṟ kaḷ perumpāṉmaiyum kaiyāḷappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl, ivvāṟu kāṇappaṭum vaṭa coṟkaḷiṉ tokai, āṅkila nūlkaḷiṟ kaiyāḷappaṭṭuk kāṇappaṭum ilattīṉ moḻic coṟkaḷiṉ tokaiyai viṭa mikutiyāṉatoṉṟaṉṟu.
eṭuttukkāṭṭāka, āṅkila viviliya nūlilum, tamiḻ viviliya nūlilum “pattuk kaṭṭaḷaikaḷ” eṉṟa pakutiyai oppumaikkāka eṭuttukkoṇṭu avaṟṟiṉ col tokuti kaḷai ārāyvōm. āṅkilac coṟkaḷai ārāyumiṭattu nēriṭaiyāṉa ilattīṉ coṟkaḷaiyum, nārmaṉ pireñcu mūlamāka vanta ilattīṉ citaivukaḷaiyum oṉṟākac cērttu ilattīṉākak kaṇakkiṭuvōm. ataṉ palaṉ kīḻvarum paṭṭikaippaṭi yākum.
col vakai	āṅkilam	tamiḻ
	mottam	ilattīṉ	tūya        mottam	  vaṭa	taṉit				āṅkilam		moḻi	tamiḻ
peyarum	43	14	29	53	21	32
peyaruriyum	20	7	13	34	7	27
viṉai
eṇṇup
peyar	5	0	5	6	1	5
“āyiram” eṉpatu vaṭamoḻip peyar. ataṉālēyē eṇṇup peyari ṉetiril vaṭamoḻiyiṉ kīḻ `1’ eṉṟu kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.
mottamākak kaṇakkiṭṭāl āṅkilattil varum ilattīṉum, tamiḻil varum vaṭamoḻiyum orēpaṭittāka nūṟṟukku 45 viḻukkāṭu ākiṟatu. iraṇṭilum iṭappeyarkaḷ, uriccoṟkaḷ, urupukaḷ, iṭaiccoṟkaḷ, ilakkaṇa uṟuppukkaḷ ivai muṟṟilum tāymoḻiyaic cērntavaiyē yākum.

āṅkilamoḻi taṉ vaḷarccimuṟaiyil tūya āṅkilō cāksaṉ moḻiyiliruntum,  ilattīṉ moḻiyiliruntum evvakaiyil utavipeṟṟirukkiṟatu eṉpatai āṅkila camayat talaivar ṭreñc7 piṉvarumāṟu viḷakkik kūṟiyuḷḷār.
“im moḻiyi (āṅkilam) ṉuṭaiya iṇaippukaḷ, oli muṟai, nāṭi, narampukaḷ, tacai nārkaḷ, iṭaccoṟkaḷ, urupukaḷ, iṭaiccoṟkaḷ, eṇṇup peyarkaḷ, tuṇai viṉaikaḷ, yāppu muṟaikkup payaṉpaṭum ciṟuciṟu acaic coṟkaḷ, ilakkaṇa muṟaiyamaippu ākiyavai ellām taṉippaṭṭa āṅkilō cāksaṉē. it teyvikak kaṭṭiṭattiṟku ilattīṉ moḻi utaviyavai ceṅkaṟkaḷum, naṉku cetukkik kaṭaicaliṭap paṭṭa calavaik kaṟkaḷumēyām; āṉāl ivai yellāvaṟṟaiyum patittup piṇaittuk kaṭṭiṭamāka eḻuppuvikka utaviya nīṟu, cuṇṇāmpu, cāntu ellām tūya āṅkilō cāksaṉē”.

ikkūṟṟu appaṭiyē tirāviṭattiṟkum vaṭamoḻikkum poruntuvatākum; mēṟ pakutiyil “āṅkilō cāksaṉ” eṉṟu varumiṭaṅkaḷi lellām, “tirāviṭam” eṉpataiyum, “ilattīṉ” eṉṟu varumiṭaṅkaḷilellām “vaṭamoḻi” eṉpataiyum amaittukkoṇṭu paṭittāl tirāviṭa moḻikkum vaṭamoḻikkum iṭaiyē ettuṇai yuṟavu irukkiṟa teṉpatu teḷḷat teḷiya viḷaṅkum.
mēṟ “pattuk kaṭṭaḷaikaḷ” eṉṟa tamiḻ viviliya nūṟpakutiyil kāṇappaṭum vaṭacoṟṟokaiyum āṅkila nūṟpakutiyil kāṇappaṭum ilattīṉ coṟṟokaiyum viyakkattakka vaṇṇam oṉṟākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟateṉpatai aṟintōm. ataṉāl āṅkilam ilattīṉukku evvaḷaviṟkuk kaṭaṉpaṭṭuḷḷatō, avvaḷaviṟkut tamiḻ vaṭamoḻikkuk kaṭaṉpaṭṭirukkavēṇṭum eṉṟu colvatu tavaṟākum. āṅkilap pakuti taṉmāṭṭuḷḷa ilattīṉ coṟkaḷai nīkkiviṭa muyaṉṟāl, ataṉ poruḷ nuṭpaṅ keṭṭuviṭum. āṉāl, tamiḻp pakuti taṉmāṭṭuḷḷa vaṭacoṟkaḷil perum pakutiyaiyō, aṉṟi muḻuvataiyumō nīkkiviṭṭut takka tamiḻc coṟkaḷaip peytukoṇṭāl evvakaiyiluṅ kuṟaivuṟātu; avvāṟu nīkkuvatāl ataṉ tūymai māṟṟum, cemmaip paṇpum uyarntoḷirumēyaṉṟik kuṟaintu maṅkā ilattīṉi liruntu peṟṟa coṟkaḷiṟ palavaṟṟiṟku āṅkilō cāksaṉil nērc coṟkaḷ kiṭaiyā; eṉavē, ac coṟkaḷai nīkkiviṭuva teṉṟāl avaṟṟiṉ karuttukkaḷai nīṇṭa, aḻakaṟṟa coṟ ṟoṭarkaḷāl tāṉ viḷakkavēṇṭum. āṉāl tamiḻō, taṉippaṭṭa vakaiyil colvaḷam niṟaintatu. appaṭiyirukka a/tēṉ vaṭacoṟkaḷai eṭuttāṇṭa teṉṟāl, colvaḷa miṉmaiyāṉaṉṟu; putumai karutiyēyām eṉṟu viṭukka. “pattuk kaṭṭaḷaikaḷ” eṉṟa viviliya nūṟpakutiyai nāṭṭuppuṟat tāḻnta makkaḷiṉ pēccu muṟaiyil amaittāl, atiluḷḷa vaṭacoṟkaḷiṉ tokai mikavuṅ kuṟaintuviṭum. ataṉaiyē cemmaippaṭuttit tirutti ilakkiya naṭaiyil amaippō māṉāl, atil eñciyuḷḷa oṉṟiraṇṭu vaṭacoṟkaḷum māyamāyp pōyviṭum. appakutiyiṟ kāṇappaṭum vaṭa coṟkaḷuḷ oṉṟē oṉṟiṟkuttāṉ kuṟṟañ collāta vakaiyil nērt tamiḻccol amaikka muṭiyātu. ac col “vikkirakam”8 eṉpatu. ac collum ataṉ karuttum tamiḻmakkaḷiṉ paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷukkum, koḷkaikaḷuṭakkum puṟampāṉavai. pārppaṉarkaḷāl purāṇak karuttukaḷōṭum, “vikkiraka ārātaṉai muṟai” eṉṉum uruva vaḻipāṭṭōṭum, ic collum karuttum tamiḻnāṭṭiṉuḷ pukuttappaṭṭaṉa vākum. pārppaṉavarkaḷiṉ camayattiṟku in nāṭṭil mutalil irunta celvākkiṉālēyē, camayak karuttukkaḷait terivippaṉavāyt tamiḻnūṟkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum coṟkaḷiṟ perumpālaṉa vaṭacoṟkaḷākavō, accoṟ citaivu kaḷākavō kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ēṟṟa tirāviṭac coṟkaḷ illai eṉpatillai; muṟṟilum ēṟṟavaiyum, orōvaḻi vaṭacoṟkaḷilum ciṟantaṉavākavuṅ koḷḷak kūṭiya coṟkaḷ uḷa. avai yellām, iṉṟu vaḻakkiḻantu, maṟaintu, ceyyuḷ vaḻakkilmaṭṭum aruki vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻk kalvi maṅkiyuḷḷa innāḷil, urainaṭaiyil ac coṟkaḷ vaḻaṅkumēl an naṭai mikavum karaṭumuraṭāṉa teṉṟum, kaṭapaṭām pōṉṟa teṉṟum karuti ikaḻappaṭum. tamiḻc camaya nūlkaḷil vaṭacoṟkaḷ potuvāka eṭuttāḷappaṭṭuḷḷamaikku ituvē, uṇmai yāṉa kāraṇamum, orē kāraṇamumākum.
ēṉaiya tirāviṭa moḻikaḷil epporuḷaik kuṟitteḻunta kaṭṭuraiyāyiṉuñ cari, ceyyuḷāyiṉuñ cari, ataṉkaṇ mikutiyāṉa vaṭacoṟkaḷ payiṉṟirukkak kāṇalām. avai putumai karuti eṭuttāḷappaṭṭaṉavalla; kiṭṭattaṭṭa iṉṟiyamaiyātaṉavākavē karutik kaiyāḷap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. inta nilaimai vantataṟkuk kāraṇam ammoḻikaḷiṉ ilakkiya vaḷarcci mutaṉmaiyākap pārppaṉarkaḷ kaiyilēyē oppaṭaikkappaṭṭu vantuḷḷamaiyēyām. teluṅku moḻiyai yeṭuttukkoṇṭāl, am moḻiyil ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷ eḻutiyavarkaḷuḷḷum, ilakkiyaṅkaḷ eḻutiyavarkaḷuḷḷum mutaṉmaiyāṉavarkaḷum, peyar pōṉavarkaḷum pārppaṉarkaḷē. ammoḻiyiṟ kāṇappaṭum nūlkaḷuḷ oṉṟē oṉṟutāṉ pārppaṉa rallātārāṉa āciriyaroruvarāl iyaṟṟappaṭṭa tākum. uṇmaiyākap pārttāl, teluṅkumakkaḷ eṉṟu aḻaippataṟku evvakaiyilum uriyarāṉa teluṅkup pārppaṉa rallāta tokutiyiṉar taṅkaḷ moḻivaḷarcci, kalaivaḷarcci ākiyavaṟṟaip pārppaṉarkaḷiṭamē oppaṭaittu viṭṭavar kaḷākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟārkaḷ.
tamiḻilō ippaṭiyillai. nūl eṉṟa peyarukkuriyaṉa vāyp pōṟṟic cēmittuvaikkavēṇṭiya vakaiyil pārppaṉar kaḷāl eḻutappaṭṭaṉavāyk kāṇakkūṭiyavai mikamikac cilavē. tamiḻmakkaḷāl avarkaḷuṭaiya tāy moḻiyākiya tamiḻiṉ vaḷarcciyum, kalaiyum, nākarikamum paṇṭutoṭṭuk kaṇṇuṅ karuttumāy ārvattuṭaṉum veṟṟiyuṭaṉum pōṟṟappaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻ ilakkiya āciriya varicaiyil pārppaṉarkaḷ eytiyatellām uraiyāciriyar eṉṟa nilaikku mēṟpaṭṭa tillai. tāḻnta vakuppiṉarāya tiruvaḷḷuvarāl eḻutappaṭṭa tirukkuṟaḷukkup pārppaṉarāṉa parimēlaḻakar eṉpār vakuttuḷḷa uraiyē mikavuñ ciṟantatoṉṟākap pārāṭṭap paṭukiṟatu.
teṉpakutiyil pēcappaṭum moḻikaḷ vaṭamoḻiyaip piṉpaṟṟiyē amaikkappaṭṭapōtilum, taṉippaṭṭa ilakkiya vaḷattai ēṟpaṭuttikkoḷḷa avaṟṟuṭ cila virumpi muyaṉṟaṉa eṉṟum, tamiḻ, teluṅku, kaṉṉaṭam, malaiyāḷam ākiya moḻikaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum mutaṉmaiyāṉa ilakkiyaṅka ḷellām vaṭamoḻi nūlkaḷiṉ moḻi peyarppō, aṉṟi virivuraiyō ātalvēṇṭum eṉṟum, av virivuraiyeḻutu vatilum vaṭamoḻit teṭarkaḷ eṭuttāḷappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa eṉṟum pērāciriyar uvilsaṉ9 eṉpavar kūṟukiṟār. tamiḻaippaṟṟiya varaiyil ik kūṟṟu oppattakkataṉṟu. tamiḻil iraṇṭu oppaṟṟa ciṟanta nūlkaḷāka ulakattōrāl karutappaṭuvaṉa kuṟaḷum, cintāmaṇiyumām. iv viraṇṭum muṟṟilum vaṭamoḻit toṭarpaṟṟavai. tamiḻāciriyarkaḷ vaṭamoḻi nūlamaippaiyum naṭaiyaiyum piṉpaṟṟiyirukkak kūṭum; toṭarukkut toṭar moḻipeyarttuḷḷaṉar eṉpatai oppuk koḷḷa muṭiyātu. tamiḻ irāmāyaṇam, māpāratam pōṉṟa kāviyaṅkaḷ ivvāṟu eḻuntavēyām. tamiḻar taṅkaḷ kamparāmāyaṇam mutaṉūlākiya vālmīki irāmāyaṇattiṉum mikavuñ ciṟantatu eṉṟu urimai pārāṭṭuvatē itaṟkuc cāṉṟākum.
(5) moḻikaḷuḷ oṉṟaṟkoṉṟaṉiṭaiyē kāṇappaṭum oṟṟumai vēṟṟumaikaḷai viḷakkap peritum payaṉpaṭum muṟaikaḷ mikavum muṭivāṉatu am moḻikaḷiṉ ilakkaṇa amaippai oppiṭṭuc cīrtūkkip pārppatēyām. am muṟaippaṭi tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ vaṭamoḻiyiṉum vēṟāṉavai yeṉṟum, tirāviṭa moḻikaḷai intu -airōppiya moḻiyiṉattōṭu iṇaippataiviṭa, cittiya moḻiyiṉat tōṭu iṇaippatē cālpuṭaittākum eṉṟum tuṇintu kūṟalām.

2.	ḻuyaracayaḷa
3.	aiṉēṭi-nuracaṭiniyan.
4.	ḷuulavāyain.
5.	kuinniḷā
6.	ṉuiuvaiṭiyēcanaiḷa.
7.	hacauhbiḷāṭiyī kūcananuh.
8.	aiayapana.
9.	ñacaṭikanaḷaḷaṭica ṟuṭaiḷaṭin

5. ilakkaṇa amaippil vaṭamoḻikkum tirāviṭa moḻikaḷukkum iṭaiyēyuḷḷa vēṟupāṭukaḷ
(i) tirāviṭa moḻikaḷil uyiraṟṟa poruḷkaḷ maṭṭumaṉṟi, pakuttaṟivaṟṟa uyirkaḷum potuppāl (a/ṟiṇai) ākavē matikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. āṇpāl, peṇpāl vakuppu, iṭap peyarkaḷ, atilum paṭarkkaiyil maṭṭumē, kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. peyaruric coṟkaḷilum, paṭarkkai viṉaikaḷilum ip paṭarkkaip peyariṉ tiripē vikutiyāy niṉṟu pāl uṇarttukiṉṟatu. uyirvakaikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ pālvakai kāṭṭavēṇṭiṉ āṇ peṇ eṉṟa peyarkaḷaic cērttēyākavēṇṭum; appōtum avai āṇ allatu peṇ pālāvatillai; potuppālēyākum. ēṉeṉil, avaṟṟaik kuṟikkum iṭappeyarkaḷum, avaṟṟiṉ payaṉilaiyāṉa  viṉaikaḷum potuppāl (a/ṟiṇai) muṭivaiyē koḷḷalvēṇṭum. ittakaiya pālvakuppu vaṭamoḻi mutaliya intu - airōppiya moḻikaḷiṉ pakaṭṭāṉa puṉaiviyal pālpākupāṭṭiṉiṉṟum muṟṟilum māṟupaṭṭatu. āṉāl cittiyak kuḻuviṉ vaḻakkukku i/tu ottatu.

(ii) tirāviṭa moḻikaḷil peyarc coṟkaḷ vēṟṟumai ēṟkumpōtu vikutiyēṟṟu urumāṟuva tillai; ac coṟkaḷiṉiṉṟu eḷitil pirikkakkūṭiya piṉ aṭaikaḷ allatu urupukaḷaiyē ēṟṟu māṟukiṉṟaṉa. mēlum paṉmaip peyariṉ vēṟṟumai yuruvāṉatu urupukaḷai ēṟkumuṉ paṉmai vikuti peṟuvatoṉṟait tavira maṟṟevvakaiyilum orumai vēṟṟumai yuruviliruntu māṟupaṭuvatillai; cittiya moḻikaḷum ivvāṟē.1
(iii) tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiṉ a/ṟiṇaip peyarkaḷukkup paṉmai perumpālum vaḻaṅkuvatillai; viṉaic collil i/tu iṉṉum arumai.
(ie) tirāviṭa moḻiyiṉ nāṉkām vēṟṟumai yurupākiya ku-ki-allatu ke vaṭamoḻi allatu intu - airōppiya (āriya) moḻikaḷiluḷḷa enta vēṟṟuimai vikutiyuṭaṉum ottatāka illai. āṉāl atē camayattil atu kīḻait turukkiyuṭaṉum2, pehistaṉ3 paṭṭayaṅkaḷuṭaṉum piṉṉiṣ moḻiyiṉaṅkaḷuṭaṉum toṭarpuṭaiya tāyirukkiṉṟatu.
(e) intu-airōppiya moḻikaḷil muṉṉurupukaḷ4 allatu oṭṭukkaḷ (pirattiyayam) payaṉpaṭuttappaṭum iṭaṅkaḷil ellām tirāviṭa moḻikaḷum, cittiyak kuḻumoḻikaḷum piṉṉurupukaḷai allatu oṭṭukkaḷaip payaṉpaṭuttukiṉṟaṉa. iv urupukaḷum, oṭṭukkaḷum ilakkaṇa muṟaippaṭi taṉiccolvakaikaḷ alla, peyarccol tiripukaḷēyām. ivvāṟē avaṟṟiṉ viṉai uriccoṟkaḷum peyar allatu toḻiṟ peyar allatu viṉaiyeccaṅkaḷē yākum. avai eppōtum tām taḻuvum viṉaikaḷukku muntiyē varukiṉṟaṉa.
(ei) vaṭamoḻiyilum, intu - airōppiya moḻikaḷilum peyaruric coṟkaḷ peyarc coṟkaḷaip pōlavē pāl, eṇ, iṭam ēṟkiṉṟaṉa. tirāviṭa moḻikaḷilum, cittiya moḻikaḷilum avai pāl mutaliyavai ēṟpatillai. ip peyaruric coṟkaḷ uṇmaiyil paṇpup peyarkaḷiliruntu vantavaiyē yākum. paṇpup peyar eṉṟu taṉippaṭa niṟkumpōtu, avai piṟa peyarkaḷ pōlavē tiṇai pāl mutaliyavai ēṟpiṉum, piṟa peyarkaḷuṭaṉ oṭṭappeṟṟu uriccollākap payaṉpaṭumpōtu aṅṅaṉam ēṟpatillai.
(eii) iṉṉoru vakaiyil tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ maṅkōliyam, mañcu mutalāṉa cittiya moḻikaḷuṭaṉ ottum intu - airōppiya moḻiyīṉattuṭaṉ māṟupaṭṭum uḷḷaṉa. a/tāvatu, iyalumpō tellām im moḻikaḷ peyaruric collai vaḻaṅkāmal, viṉaiccolliṉ peyarecca urupaiyē vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. (uyar maram - eṉpataṟku uyarnta maram eṉpatupōla eṉka.) ivvaḻakkattiṉ payaṉāyp paṇpup peyarkaḷaip peyaruriyāka vaḻaṅkumpōtu kūṭa, ataṉuṭaṉ peyaruriccol vikutiyāka `āṉa’ eṉṟa peyareccam cērttukkoḷḷappaṭukiṟatu. (civappāṉa)
(eii) taṉmaip paṉmaiyil muṉṉilaiyai uṭpaṭuttiyum uṭpaṭuttāmalum iraṇṭu vakaikaḷ tirāviṭa moḻikkum, cittiya moḻikaḷukkumē ciṟappāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. vaṭamoḻi yilum, intu -airōppiya moḻiyilum iṅṅaṉam illai. itaṉai yoppatāy oruvāṟu kuṟikkakkūṭiyatu piṟkuṟitta moḻikaḷiluḷḷa irumai5 eṉṟa eṇṇēyākum.
(io) tirāviṭa moḻikaḷil ceyappāṭṭu viṉaikkut taṉi vaṭivam kiṭaiyātu. paṭu eṉṉum tuṇaiviṉai cērkkappaṭṭē ap poruḷil vaḻaṅkappaṭukiṟatu.
(o) tirāviṭa moḻikaḷum, cittiya moḻikaḷum intu - airōppiya moḻikaḷaip pōla iṇaippu iṭaic coṟkaḷaip6 payaṉpaṭuttāmal “ceytu” eṉṟa viṉaiyeccattaiyē vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
(oi) uṭaṉpāṭu, etirmaṟai eṉṟa iv viraṇṭu viṉai vaṭivaṅkaḷiruppatu tirāviṭa moḻikaṭkum, vaṭamoḻikkum iṭaiyē kāṇappaṭum talaiyāya vēṟṟumaikaḷuḷ oṉṟu. cittiya moḻikaḷuṭaṉ tirāviṭa moḻikaḷukku uḷḷa oppumaikaḷil itu talaimaiyāṉatākak kāṇkiṟatu.
(oii) tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ, maṅkōliyam, mañcu, mutaliyavai, ōraḷavukkup piṟa cittiya moḻikaḷ ākiya ivaṟṟiṉ taṉippaṭṭa ciṟappup paṇpukaḷuḷ oṉṟu iṇaippu iṭappeyarkaḷiṉiṭamākap peyareccaṅkaḷ7 vaḻaṅkuvatām. ippeyareccam mukkālaṅkaḷilum uḷḷa viṉaiyeccattuṭaṉ āṟām vēṟṟumai urupōṭotta a-eṉṉum cāriyai peṟṟu vantatu ākum. tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ etaṉilum iṇaippu iṭappeyar eṉpataṉ niḻalkūṭak kāṇappaṭavillai. kōṇṭu moḻiyil maṭṭum viṉaiyeccaṅkaḷ illāmal oḻintu pōṉatai oṭṭi, hinti moḻiyiṉ iṇaippu iṭappeyar kaiyāḷappaṭṭu varukiṟatu.

(oiii) tirāviṭa moḻikaḷilum, cittiya moḻikaḷilum taḻuvu moḻikaḷum, taḻuvu toṭarkaḷum taḻuvappaṭum moḻikkup piṉṉatākavē varukiṉṟaṉa. intu - airōppiya moḻi iṉattil avai potuvāka muṉvarukiṉṟaṉa. eṉavē, im moḻikaḷ aṉaittilum vākkiyattiṉ mutalil eḻuvāyum, muṭivil payaṉilaiyum, eḻuvāy aṭai8 eḻuvāykku muṉṉum, iṭaiyil ceyappaṭuporuḷum viṉai aṭaikaḷum ākavarukiṉṟaṉa; peyaraṭaikaḷum peyareccaṅkaḷum peyariṉ āṟām vēṟṟumaiyum peyarukku muṉṉum, iṭa urupukaḷum kāla urupukaḷum peyarukku muṉ aṉṟit tām taḻuvum peyarkaḷukkum avaṟṟiṉ vēṟṟumai urupukaḷukkum piṉṉākavum varukiṉṟaṉa. viṉai yeccaṅkaḷum viṉai aṭaikaḷ pōla viṉaikku muṉṉākiṉṟaṉa. ellāvaṟṟaiyum taḻuvum viṉaimuṟṟu iṟutiyilēyē niṟkiṟatu. ivvāṟu collamaippu, coṟṟoṭaramaippu muṟaiyilum cittiya moḻikaḷ tirāviṭa moḻikaḷai ottē irukkiṉṟaṉa.
mēṟkūṟiya ilakkaṇa amaitikaḷilellām tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ potuvāka intu - airōppiya moḻikaḷiṉiṉṟum vēṟupaṭṭē iruppatum, neṭunāḷ kalantu vāḻntuṅkūṭa vaṭamoḻiyiṉiṉṟum iṉṉum vēṟupaṭṭē yiruppatum kāṇka. atē camayattil, eṅkeṅkō naṭu āciyāvilum, vaṭaāciyāvilum citaṟik kiṭakkum cittiya moḻikaḷuṭaṉ avai porunti iruppatu kavaṉikkat takkatu.
tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ vēṟu, vaṭamoḻi vēṟu eṉpatu mēṟkūṟiyavaṟṟāl naṉku teriyappaṭum. tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ vaṭamoḻiyiliruntu peṟappaṭṭaṉa alla; aṉṟi ataṉuṭaṉ cērkkappaṭakkūṭiya paṇpuṭaiyaṉavumalla.
cila kuṟippiṭṭa iṭaṅkaḷil vaṭamoḻi piṟa intu - airōppiya moḻikaḷaiviṭac cittiya moḻik kuḻuvuṭaṉ uṟavuṭaiyatāyt tōṟṟuvatu oru vēḷaip paḻaṅkuṭikaḷuṭaṉ uḷḷa neṭunāṭ paḻakkattaip poṟuttatāyirukkalām. ivaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu, vaṭamoḻiyīl (ṭa ṇa ṣa) eṉṟa nāaṭi aṇṇa meykaḷ iruppaṉavākum. iv vakaiyil, “moḻiyiyalil cīṉattiṉ iṭam”9 eṉṟa nūlil eṭkiṉs eṉpār koṇṭa putiya koḷkaiyaik kuṟippiṭal vēṇṭum. urupiṉṟi vikutiyilēyē peyarkaḷiṉ vēṟṟumai amaital colloḻuṅku, taḻuvu vākkiyaṅkaḷ talaivākkiyattiṟku muṉvaral, viṉaimuṟṟu iṟutiyil niṟṟal ākiya ilakkaṇa amaippukkaḷuḷ vaṭamoḻi āriya iṉattaiviṭat turāṉiya iṉattaiyē piṉpaṟṟiyuḷḷatu eṉa avar kūṟukiṟār. āṉāl, vaṭamoḻiyai viṭa ivvakaiyil iṉṟaiya vaṭa intiya uṇṇāṭṭu moḻikaḷē vilakkiṉṟi muṟṟilum iv voḻuṅkaip piṉpaṟṟukiṉṟaṉa. vaṭamoḻi urainaṭaiyilkūṭa oṉṟiraṇṭiṭaṅkaḷil iv voḻuṅkukku māṟupāṭu ēṟpaṭukiṟateṉpatai eṭkiṉsē eṭuttukkāṭṭukiṟār. āyiṉum potuppaṭa urainaṭaiyil iv voḻuṅkuppaṭi viṉaimuṟṟē īṟṟilum, taḻuvu vākkiyaṅkaḷ muṉṉum varukiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl, ik koḷkai nām ēṟṟukkoḷḷattakka uṟutippāṭu uṭaiyataṉṟu; ēṉeṉil col oḻuṅku oṉṟaiyēpaṟṟi vakaippaṭuttuvatāyiṉ kirēkka moḻiyaiviṭa ilattīṉum, āṅkilattaiviṭa jermaṉum turāṉiyac cārpuṭaiyavai eṉṟu kūṟavēṇṭi varum.
aṭikkuṟippukaḷ
1.	vaṭamoḻiyilum, intu-airōppiya moḻikaḷilum orumaikkum, paṉmaikkum ovvoru vēṟṟumaiyilum vevvēṟu vikutikaḷ uḷa.
2.	ṭīcanaivēyaṭakūracamaiḷā.
3.	kṣanāḷaivaran kūyaṭenavaḷa.
4.	ñacanayīṭiḷavaiiṭin
5.	orumai paṉmai eṉpatiṉṟi orumai, irumai, paṉmai eṉṟu eṉ mūṉṟu vaḻaṅkum. 
6.	ūṭitēranuvaiṭiḷē.
7.	cunaṭayavaiena ñacaṭinṭiraḷē.
8.	haṉataranuvaḷa.
9.	“ūhiyē’ḷa yīṭayauna in ñāṭaiṭiṭaṭipala” - āca. nuṉamaiḷē.
6. tirāviṭamum vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷum
tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ vaṭamoḻiyiliruntu peṟappaṭṭavaiyē, avaṟṟuḷ vaṭamoḻic cārpaṟṟaṉavākak kāṇakkiṭakkum coṟkaḷ yātō oru paṇṭaip pākata moḻiyiliruntu peṟappaṭṭavai eṉṟu koṇṭirunta koḷkaikaḷ iraṇṭum piḻaipaṭṭaṉa veṉṟu kāṭṭappaṭṭatu. eṉavē, aṟiñar cilar ivaṟṟiṟku nērmāṟāṉa koḷkai yoṉṟaik kūṟa muṉvantaṉar. vaṭamoḻiyiliruntu kiḷaitta vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷuḷ kāṇappeṟum piṟamoḻik kalapput tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiṉ kūṭṭuṟavāl ēṟpaṭṭatutāṉ eṉpatu ip putuk koḷkai. ik koḷkaiyaip parappiyavarkaḷil talaiyāṉōr pampāyaic cārnta rereṇṭ ṭākṭar sṭīveṉcaṉ1 eṉpavarum, nēpāḷattaic cārnta hāṭjsaṉ2 eṉparuvamē. ivarkaḷ karuttu (1) vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷ iyaṟkait tākkutalkaḷālum, kāla māṟupāṭṭālum vaṭamoḻiyiliruntu kiḷaittavaiyalla; āṉāl, vaṭamoḻiyallāta piṟa moḻikaḷiṉ āṟṟal mikka kalappiṉāl citaivuṟṟaṉavēyām. (2) ip piṟamoḻikaḷ tirāviṭap pēccuṭaṉ oppumaiyuṭaiyaṉa; it tirāviṭap pēccē, paṇṭai niṣātarkaḷ pēccum, piṟa paḻaṅkālak kuṭikaḷiṉ pēccumākum eṉpatē.
ivviru pakutikaḷuḷ muṉṉaiyatu piṉṉaiyataiviṭa uṟutippāṭuṭaiyatākum; āṉāl atuvum oruvāṟu mikaipaṭak kūṟappaṭṭateṉṟum, ataṉait tiruttiyamaikka vēṇṭum eṉṟum tōṉṟukiṉṟatu. ēṉeṉiṟ kūṟutum: mutaṉmaiyāṉa cilavaṟṟai nōkka vaṭamoḻi hintiyākavum, vaṅkāḷamākavun tiripuṟṟatu iyaṟkai māṟutalē eṉṟu niṟuvappaṭṭuḷḷatu. airōppāvil ilattīṉiliruntu eppaṭi ittāliyamum, spāṉiṣum kiḷaittaṉavō, appaṭiyētāṉ ivaiyum. āṉāl, vaṭamoḻi ilakkaṇa amaippōṭum paṇpuc ciṟappukkaḷōṭum, vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷiṉ ilakkaṇa amaippaiyum paṇpuc ciṟappukkaḷaiyum oppiṭṭu nōkkiṉāl, avaṟṟiṭaiyē kāṇappaṭum vēṟṟumaikaḷ āriyac cārpaṟṟa tākkutalkaḷiṉ payaṉē eṉṟu oruvāṟu teḷiyalām.
atilum, palvēṟupaṭṭa vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷu ḷellām orēpaṭittāṉa ilakkaṇa vēṟṟumaikaḷ kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟamai koṇṭu, itaṟkuk kāraṇam potuppaṭṭa oru tākkutalākavē irukkavēṇṭum eṉpatu eḷitil ūkikkap paṭum. “nākarika kālattiṟku muṟpaṭṭa paḻaṅkālac cuvaṭukaḷākiya paṇṭaiya, cemmaippaṭāta muraṭṭu moḻiccoṟkaḷē” itaṟkuk kāraṇam eṉṟu pērāciriyar uvilsaṉ kūṟukiṟār. potuvākavē, vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷuḷ ittakaiya tākkutal pattil orupaṅkum, marātti moḻiyil maṭṭum aintil orupaṅkuntāṉ eṉṟu kaṇakkiṭappaṭṭuḷḷamaiyāl, āriyac cārpaṟṟa kalappē kāraṇam eṉpatu naṉku teḷiyappaṭum.
vaṭamoḻi pēciya pārppaṉarkaḷ, kṣattiriyarkaḷ, vaiciyarkaḷ eṉṟa vakuppiṉarkaḷaṭaṅkiya āriyarkaḷ varukaikku muṉṉē, vaṭa intiyāviṉ perum pakutiyil “tasyūkkaḷ, niṣātar, milēccar” eṉṟu avarkaḷāl aḻaikkappaṭṭu vanta paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷē vāḻntu vantiruntaṉar eṉpatum, am makkaḷ cittiya iṉattaiyō, aṉṟi āriyac cārpaṟṟa vēṟōr iṉattaiyō cērntavarkaḷ eṉpatum aṉaivarālum oppukkoḷḷappaṭṭa uṇmaikaḷākum. ip paḻaṅkuṭimakkaḷ pēciya tiruntāta cittiya moḻic, coṟkaḷ, paṭaiyeṭuttu vantu veṟṟipeṟṟa āriyarkaḷ tam colvaḷamum, poruṇmaiyum niṟainta vaṭamoḻi veḷḷattil āḻntu pōyviṭutal iyalpē. eṉiṉum, ap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷiṉ moḻi orēyaṭiyāy aḻintoḻiyāmal, taṉṉai viḻuṅkavanta vaṭamoḻiyaiyē uraṅkoṇṭu tākkik kalakkit taṉṉuṭaiya coṟkaḷiṟ cilavaṟṟaip pukuttip putiyatoru matukaiyaiyum naṭaip pōkkaiyum atu mēṟkoḷḷac ceytirukkavēṇṭu meṉpatu piṉvaruṅ kāraṇaṅkaḷāl ūkikkappaṭum: (1) cittiya moḻiyiṉ ilakkaṇa amaippu nilaiyāṉatum, etirppukku īṭukōṭukkum āṟṟalum vāyntatu. (2) āriyarkaḷai nōkka ap paḻaṅkuṭikaḷ tokaiyil mikuntavarkaḷā yiruntaṉar. (3) ataṉāl, avarkaḷai āriyarāl aṟavē aḻittoḻikka muṭiyavillai. (4) mutaṟkaṇ aṭimaikaḷākkappaṭṭu nāḷā vaṭṭattil āriyak kūṭṭattiṉaruṭaṉē cērkkappaṭṭaṉar.
ik koḷkai maṟṟek koḷkaikaiyum viṭa iṉṟaiya nilaimaiyait teḷivupaṭa viḷakkukiṟatu. vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷuḷ coltokutiyilmaṭṭumē vaṭa moḻic cārpu mikutiyāyiruppatum, ilakkaṇa amaippu cittiya moḻic cārpuṭaiyatā yiruppatum, nōkka., ammoḻikaḷ cittiyap pakutiyuṭaṉ kalanta āriya aṭippaṭaiyuṭaiyavai eṉpataiviṭa, āriyattuṭaṉ kalanta cittiya aṭippaṭaiyai uṭaiya moḻikaḷ eṉṟu kūṟuvatē mikavum poruttamuṭaiyatākum. im moḻikaḷuḷ “tāttāriya allatu cālṭiya”3 (atāvatu cittiya)k kalappu uḷḷatu eṉa mutaṉmutal kūṟiyavar car. uvilliyam jōṉs4 āvar. itaṉai maṟuppavaruḷ talaiyāṉavar hinti moḻiyaip paṟṟi, 1872 ēpril iṇṭiyaṉ āṇṭikkavari5 eṉṟa veḷiyīṭṭil kaṭṭurai oṉṟeḻutiya kiraus pi.si.es6. āvar. avar hintimoḻi oṉṟai maṭṭum ārāyntuḷḷār; atilum ilakkaṇa amaippai viṭṭuc col tokuti oṉṟai maṭṭumē ārāyntuḷḷār. ākaiyāl iṉṉum ittuṟaiyil nuṇuki ārāyntu muṭipu kāṇum varaiyil, cittiya aṭippaṭai muṭipē nilavi varum.
iṉi, ṭākṭar sṭīveṉsaṉ koḷkaiyiṉ piṉṉaiya pakutiyai nōkkuvōm. vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷil vaṭa moḻiyallāta piṟa moḻik kalappu irukkiṟateṉpatu oppukkoḷḷappaṭiṉ, ap piṟamoḻi tirāviṭa moḻi yiṉattaic cērntatē eṉpatu ap pakutiyākum. ik koḷkai avvaḷavu tiṭṭamāka ārāyntu niṟuvappaṭuvatoṉṟaṉṟu. (vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷāyiṉuñ cari, teṉṉintiya moḻi kaḷāyiṉuñ cari,) ellāvaṟṟuḷḷum, cittiya moḻik kalappō, tirāviṭa moḻik kalappō perumpālum orē paṭittāṉatu. (2) āriyarāl mutalil kaippaṟṟap peṟṟa vaṭa intiyak kōṭṭaṅkaḷaḷ ak kalappiṉ aḷavu mikavuṅ kuṟukiyatu. (3) caṟṟuc cēymaiyiluḷḷa  takkāṇam, teliṅkāṇam, maicūr ākiya iṭaṅkaḷil ak kalappiṉ aḷavu caṟṟup perukiyatu. (4) nila mūkkiṉ teṉkōṭiyil iruppatum, maṉuviṉ kālattilum, irāmāyaṇa kālattilum pārppaṉa ātikkam kārttikaip piṟaipōl iruntuvarappeṟṟatumākiya tamiḻ nāṭṭilō atu mikap parantu perukiyiruntatu.
mutaṟkaṇ, immuṭipu paṇṭaiya intiya varalāṟṟiṉ pōkkōṭu ottatākavē kāṇappaṭum. eṉiṉum, moḻi yārāyccik kaṇṇāṭikoṇṭu pārkkiṉ vaṭa intiyap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷum, teṉṉintiyap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷum orē iṉattār eṉṟu colliviṭuvataṟkillai. vaṭaintiya moḻikaḷiṉ ilakkaṇa amaippu cittiya moḻikaḷōṭu ottiruppatāṉālum, tirāviṭa moḻikaḷuṭaṉ ciṟappāka ottiruppatākak kāṇappaṭavillai. vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷuḷ kāṇappeṟum  vaṭamoḻic cārpaṟṟa pakuti tirāviṭa moḻi kaḷōṭu evvaḷavu toṭarpuṭaiyatāk kāṇappaṭukiṟatō, avvaḷaviṟkuk kīḻait turukkiya moḻikaḷuṭaṉō, aṉṟic cittiya moḻikaḷuṭaṉōṭu toṭarpuṭaiyatākavuṅ kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ātalāl mēṟkoḷkai eḷitil oppuk koḷḷak kūṭiyatoṉṟāka illai.
vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷiṉ ilakkaṇa amaippiṟkum, tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiṉ ilakkaṇa amaippiṟkum uḷḷa talaimaiyāṉa oppumaikaḷ kīḻvarupavaiyām:
(1) peyarccoṟkaḷiṉpiṉ urupukaḷ cērntu vēṟṟumaikaḷ uṇṭātal. (2) paṉmaip peyarkaḷiṉ vēṟṟumai orumaiyiṉiṉṟum vēṟupaṭāmal paṉmai vikutiyai mutalil cērttup piṉ vēṟṟumai urupukaḷaic cērppataṉāl uṇṭātal. (3) vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷ palavaṟṟuḷ tirāviṭa moḻikaḷaip pōlavē taṉmaip paṉmaiyiṉ muṉṉilaiyai uḷappaṭutti oṉṟum, vilakki oṉṟumāka iraṇṭu vaṭivaṅkaḷ iruttal. (40 urupukaḷum, oṭṭukkaḷum peyarukkumuṉ varāmal piṉvarutal. (5) viṉaiccolliṉ kālattai iṭainilai mutaliya uṟuppukaḷ kāṭṭal. (6) taḻuvu vākkiyaṅkaḷ talaimai vākkiyattiṉ muṉ varutal. (7) taḻuvum moḻi taḻuvappaṭum moḻikkup piṉ varutal.
vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷ tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ ākiyavaṟṟiṉ ilakkaṇa amaippil ittakaiya oppumaikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉpatu uṇmaiyē. āṉāl, ivaṟṟāl iv viraṇṭu moḻiyiṉaṅkaḷukkum nērāṉa uṟavu uḷateṉak kūṟiviṭutaṟkillai. ēṉeṉil, iv voppumaikaḷ tirāviṭa moḻikaḷōṭumaṭṭuma ciṟappākak kāṇappaṭum oṉṟumaik kaḷākāmal, cittiya iṉattaic cērnta moḻiyiṉaṅkaḷ palavaṟṟōṭum, orē vakaiyil, orē aḷavil kāṇappaṭum potuppaṭaiyāṉa oppumaikaḷākiṉṟaṉa. turukkiya maṅkōliya moḻikaḷukkum, tirāviṭa moḻikaḷukkum iṭaiyē kāṇap paṭum vēṟṟumaikaḷil yātoṉṟum, vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭavillai. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka, cittiya moḻiyiṉaṅ kaḷilillāta peyareccattai eṭuttuk koḷvōm. itu koṭu oṉṟu nīṅkalāka ēṉaiya tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ ellā vaṟṟilum uḷatu; āṉāl vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷ oṉṟilum illai.
ataip pōṉṟē, tirāviṭa moḻikaḷil ellā viṉaittiripukaḷukkum oḻuṅkāka etirmaṟaiviṉai irukkiṉṟatu. avaṟṟuḷ etuvum vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷil illai. tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum iṭappeyar eṇṇup peyarkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟuḷ oṉṟutāṉum avaṟṟuḷ illai. āṉāl, intiyāvukku appāl pehistaṉ paṭṭaṅkaḷilum cīṉa moḻiyilum, ostiyakku7 moḻiyilum, lāppar8 moḻi yiluṅ kūṭa ivaṟṟuṭ cila kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. mēlum, im moḻikaḷil tirāviṭat tākku ētēṉum iruntirukkak kūṭumāyiṉ kai, kāl, kaṇ, kātu (cevi) pōṉṟa mutaṉmaiyāṉa cila coṟkaḷēṉum, avaṟṟiṉ citaivukaḷēṉum ivaṟṟuḷ em moḻiyilāyiṉum kāṇappaṭāmalirukka iṭamillai. uṇmaiyil attakaiya ec collum ivaṟṟuḷ yāṇṭuṅ kāṇappaṭavillai. pērāciriyar sṭīveṉsaṉ kaṇṭa cila oppumaikaḷ aruvaḻakkāyeṅkō mūlaiyilirun teṭukkappaṭṭa coṟkaḷ  ākum. vēṟu mutaṟcoṟkaḷum, ilakkaṇa amaiti oppumaikaḷum illāviṭattu ac ciṟupāṉmaiyāṉa oppumaikaḷaik koṇṭu oru muṭivukku varamuṭiyātu. ākavē, mutaṉmaiyāṉa vērc coṟkaḷaip poṟutta varaiyil tirāviṭa moḻikaḷukkum, vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷukkumiṭaiyē ettuṇai vēṟupāṭu! āṉāl tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ ellā vaṟṟuḷḷum ettuṇai oṟṟumai! viyakkattakka iv vuṇmaikaḷaik kīḻvarum aṭṭavaṇaiyiṟ kāṇalām; taṉmai muṉṉilai yorumaip peyar, iṭap peyarkaḷuḷ eḻuvāyil ōr uruvum, piṟa vēṟṟumaikaḷil vēṟu uruvum, viṉai vikutikaḷil maṟṟōruruvumāka māṟi varuvatuṭaṉ, orē iṭattilum urukkaḷ paṇṭaik kālattil oruvakaiyākavum, piṟkālattil vēṟākavum  irukkiṉṟamaiyāl vēṇṭiya iṭaṅkaḷil avai yāvum  oppumaiyākat tarappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
(piṉvarum aṭṭavaṇaikaḷil eḷitil oppiṭṭaṟitaṟkāka vaṭamoḻi iṭappeyarkaḷum, turukkiya iṭappeyarkaḷum piṟaikkuṟikaḷiṭṭu mutaliṟ koṭukkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.)

kaura moḻikaḷiṉ iṭap peyarkaḷukkum tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiṉ iṭap peyarkaḷukkum malaikkum maṭuvukkumuḷḷa vēṟṟumaiyiruppatai nōkka, `am moḻikaḷiṉ tirivukku mūlakāraṇamākat tākkutalkaḷ vēṟevaiyāyiṉum ākakkūṭum, tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ ākā, eṉpatuteṟṟeṉap pulappaṭukiṉṟatu. āṉāl, atē iṭap peyarkaḷiṉ ārāycci yālēyē pala vaṭamoḻikaḷilum cittiya vikutiyākiya ṉakaram iruppatu kāṇalām. ataṉōṭu, taṉmaiyil vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷ oṉṟilēṉum vaṭamoḻi eḻuvāyuru illāmal, turukkiya moḻiyiṉ eḻuvāyaip pōṉṟa mai(ṉ) allatu ma(ṉ) eṉṟa cittiya tōṟṟa muṭaiya uruvē nilavutal kāṇka. oruvēḷai etirkāla ārāyccikaḷāl tirāviṭa uṟavu uṇṭeṉṟu ēṟpaṭṭālum ēṟpaṭalām. eṉavē, vaṭaintiya moḻikaḷiṉ vaṭamoḻic cārpaṟṟa pakutiyum, teṉ intiya moḻikaḷum iraṇṭum āriyac cārpaṟṟavai eṉpatum, iraṇṭum cittiyac cārpuṭaiyavai eṉpatun tavira vēṟu evvakaiyilum oppumaiyuṭaiyaṉa alla eṉṟē tuṇiyattakum.
iṉi, ṭākṭar kuṇṭerṭ vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷil maṭṭumalla, vaṭa moḻiyilēkūṭa, tirāviṭat tākkuk kāṇap paṭukiṟatu eṉkiṟār9. inti moḻiyai ārāynta kiraus eṉpavarō, vaṭamoḻic cārpaṟṟa pakuti yeṉṟoṉṟu avvaḷavā yiruntirukka vaḻiyillai eṉṟu kūṟi, ataṟkuk kīḻvarum kāraṇaṅkaḷaik kāṭṭukiṟār: (1) ammoḻiyiṉ paḻamaiyāṉa ilakkaṇa nūlār ataṉaik kuṟippiṭātatu. (2) vaṭamoḻic cārpaṟṟateṉa viraintu tuṇiyappaṭṭa coṟkaḷ, nuṇṇiya ārāycciyāl, vaṭamoḻi yeṉṟu perumpālum niṟuvappaṭṭu varukiṉṟamai. (3) vaṭamoḻi vaḷarcciyuṟṟa kālattil, piṟamoḻit tākkutalkaḷāl vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷ tiripuṟṟamaikku eṉṉeṉṉa niyatikaḷ kāṇappaṭṭaṉavō an niyatikaḷai ikkāla vaṭanāṭṭu moḻikaḷum piṉpaṟṟi varukiṉṟamai.
icceytiyaik kuṟittu 1872il pīms eṉpār “āriyac cārpaṟṟa ikkāla intiya moḻikaḷiṉ oppilakkaṇam”10 eṉṟa nūlil piṉvarumāṟu viḷakkiyuḷḷār: “iṉi, vaṭamoḻiyu mallāta āriyamumallāta coṟkaḷaip paṟṟi ārāyvōm. āriyar intiyāvukku vantapōtu tamakkumuṉ aṅkuk kūṭiyirunta makkaḷōṭu neṭunāḷ pōriṭṭu nāṭṭai avarkaḷiṭamiruntu paṟittāka vēṇṭiyiruntatu. attakaiya pōrkaḷukkiṭaiyē amaiti nilaviya kālamum iruntiruttal vēṇṭum. appōtu iru tiṟattiṉarum kalantu oruvaruṭaṉ oruvar karuvippōr ceyyātu, vāḻkkaip pōr naṭāttiyiruppar. āriyarallātā riṭaiyē āriyaratu tākkē mikutiyāyiruntiruppiṉum, āriyariṭaiyē āriyarallātār tākkam oraḷaviṟku illā tirukka muṭiyātu. ākavē, vaṭamoḻiyilēyē āriyac cārpaṟṟaṉavākat tōṉṟum coṟkaḷ cila kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉamai iyalpē. ik kāla vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷilō ic coṟkaḷ mikutiyākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. eṉavē, ec coṟkaḷ āriyac coṟkaḷākat tōṟṟavillaiyō avaiyellām āriyac cārpaṟṟavaiyē eṉṟu kūṟa muṉvaruvatu vaḻakkamāki viṭṭatu.
“iṅṅaṉam āriyarkaḷ paḻaṅ kuṭikaḷiṭamiruntu kaṭaṉ vāṅkiṉar eṉṟa koḷkaiyai oppukkoḷvataṟkut taṭaikaḷ pala uḷḷaṉa. moḻiyiyaliṟ piṟa cāṉṟukaḷiṉ tuṇaiyillāta viṭattu veṟum colloppumai koṇṭu etaiyum tuṇintuviṭa muṭiyātu. pala vaḻikaḷilum ciṟanta pēraṟiñarkūṭa it tuṟaiyil, “āṉaikkum nāṉku kāl, pūṉaikkum nāṉku kāl; āṉaik kālilum pūṉaik kālilum nakaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa” eṉṟa oppumaikaḷiṉ pēril muṭivukaṭṭiviṭukiṉṟaṉar. oru tamiḻc collil oru pakaram irukkiṟatu; oru vaṭacollilum oru pakaram irukkiṟatu. ākavē, vaṭacol tamiḻc colliliruntu vantatu eṉṟu kūṟiviṭuvatā! mutalāvatāka, intiyap paḻaṅkuṭikaḷaiviṭa uṭalvaliyilum aṟivuvaṉmaiyilum āriyarkaḷ uyarvuṭaiyavarkaḷākavē iruntaṉar. eṉavē, avarkaḷ ēṟṟataiviṭa īntatē mikutiyā yiruntirukka vēṇṭum. iraṇṭāvatutāka, intiyāvukku varuvataṉ muṉṉarē āriyarkaḷ colvaḷa mikkatoru moḻiyai uṭaiyavarāyiruntaṉar. eṉavē, uṭai, karuvikaḷ, taṭṭumuṭṭukaḷ, kaṉṟu kālikaḷ, uṭaṟ kūṟukaḷ, uṟavu muṟaikaḷ pōṉṟa potuk karuttukkaḷukkuriya coṟkaḷaikkūṭa avarkaḷ kaṭaṉvāṅka vēṇṭum nilaimai avarkaḷiṭam iruntirukkamuṭiyātu. avarkaḷ kaṭaṉ vāṅkavēṇṭu meṉṟāl putiya ceṭi koṭikaḷ, uyir vakaikaḷ, tām muṉṉark kaṇṭirāta putup poruḷkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟiṉ peyarkaḷaiyē yākum. ivaṟṟilkūṭat tam moḻic coṟkaḷaiyē tirittum kūṭṭiyum putuc coṟkaḷai ākkik koḷḷum āṟṟal ellā moḻikaḷukkum potuvāya amaintuḷḷatu. mūṉṟāvatāka, nila iyalaip11 poṟuttu eḻum cikkalkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. āriyarvarukaiyiṉpōtu avarkaḷuṭaṉ naṭpākavō pākaiyākavō uṟavupūṇṭa makkaḷ yār yār? vantēṟiya āriyarka ḷellōrumē tirāviṭarkaḷuṭaṉ neruṅki uṟavāṭi irukkamuṭiyumā? muṭiyumeṉṟāl eppoḻutu? eppaṭi? iṉṟaiya ārāycci nilaiyil iv viṉākkaḷukkut tiṟampaṭa viṭaiyiṟuppatu aritu. eṉiṉum, avaṟṟiṟku ētāvatu oru vaḻi kāṇal vēṇṭum. kaṅkaik karaiyiṉiṉṟum āriyarāl teṟkē viraṭṭappaṭṭa makkaḷ kōlēriyarum, vaṭakkē viraṭṭap paṭṭavar tipēttuk kalappumakkaḷum āvar. eṉavē, kōlēriyar moḻic cāṉṟiṉṟi vaṭamoḻic col oṉṟu nērākat tamiḻiliruntu peṟappaṭṭu viṭṭatu eṉpatai oppuk koḷḷutal cālātu. ittakaiya nilaimaiyil ac colliṉ mūlattai ārāyum pakuti uṇmaiyil vaṭamoḻiyum, ataṉ kiḷaimoḻikaḷum eṉṟa aḷavukkē kuṟukiviṭu meṉpataṟkaiya millai. āḻntu nōkkiṉāl atil perumpālum ataṟkuriya vērccollaik kāṇalām.”
ik kūṟṟu oruvāṟu oppukkoḷḷak kūṭiyatēyām. tirāviṭa moḻikaḷaip paṟṟiyavarai vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷil avaṟṟiṉ tākkutal oruciṟitutāṉ iruntirukkak kūṭum. āṉāl, cittiyat tākkutalkaḷālō aṉṟi āriyac cārpaṟṟa piṟa moḻikaḷiṉ tākkutalkaḷālō, vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷ iṉṟaiya tirinta nilaiyai aṭaintirukkak kūṭāvō eṉpatu vēṟu kēḷvi. tirāviṭam, cikattiyam, āriyac cārpaṟṟavai eṉpaṉa oru poruṭ kiḷavikaḷalla. pīms eṉpār `uyir olimāṟṟaṅkaḷ’12 eṉṟa pirivil, “āriya uyirolikaḷ intiyāvukku vaṭakkilum kiḻakkilum naṭuvilum teṟkilum uḷḷa veḷi iṉattārāl evvaḷavu māṟutalaṭaintaṉa veṉṟu collamuṭiyātu. āṉāl, māṟutal aṭaintuḷḷaṉa eṉpatu maṭṭum maṟukkamuṭiyāta uṇmaiyākum” eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār. vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷuḷ akaramum ākāramum ēkāramāka māṟiyamai, ittakaiya āriyac cārpaṟṟa  māṟutal eṉṟu avar kūṟukiṟār. nāvaṭi aṇṇa meyyākiya ḷakarattaip paṟṟik kūṟukaiyil, atu rakara ṭakaraṅkaḷuṭaṉ mayaṅkukiṉṟatu eṉṟum, atu naṭu intiyāviluḷḷa kōlar kaḷiṉ kuḻuvil kāṇappaṭukiṟatu eṉṟum avar uraikkiṉṟār. ik kōlarkaḷuṭaṉum tirāviṭarkaḷuṭaṉum neṭunāḷ uṟavu koṇṭirunta marāṭṭiya, oriyā makkaḷiṭai, inta ḷakaram mikutiyākap payilvatu viyappaṉṟu eṉṟum avar kūṟukiṟār.
“cinti moḻiyiṉ ilakkaṇam”13 eṉṟa nūlil ṭākṭar erṉasṭu ṭrampu āriyac cārpaṟṟa allatu tāttāriya moḻiyiṉat tākkutalkaḷ vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa’ eṉṟu uṟutiyākak kūṟukiṟār; muṉ nā aṇpal oliyākiya takara nakara sakaraṅkaḷiṉiṭamāka, avai nāaṭi aṇṇa oliyākiya ṭakara ṇakara ṣakaraṅkaḷaip peritum ēṟkiṉṟamai ataṟkuc cāṉṟu pakarum eṉṟum kūṟukiṟār. atē pōṉṟu uyirppu oliyākiya ha eṉpataip pirākiruta moḻi vilakkuvatu nōkka, “ataip pēciya potu makkaḷiṭaiyē tāttāriyat tākku iruntirukkavēṇṭum. teṟkēyuḷḷa tirāviṭa moḻikaḷil iv eḻuttu illāmai kāṇka” eṉṟum kūṟukiṟār. mēlum, tirāviṭa moḻiyiṉattaic cērnta teluṅku `cca’, `ja’ eṉṟa eḻuttukkaḷai `ṭca’ eṉṟum, ṭsa eṉṟum olippatu pōlavē marāṭṭi moḻiyum olikkiṉṟatu; ātalāl tirāviṭat tākku marāṭṭiyiluḷḷatu veḷippaṭai eṉṟum avar muṭivukaṭṭukiṟār.
aṭikkuṟippukaḷ
1.	cunae ṉuca. ḷuvanaenaḷēṭin ṭika kṣaṭiayela - tuṭiracayēṭa ṭika vāna haḷayaivaiu ḷuṭiunaivala ṭika kṣaṭiayela..
2.	āca. ḻaṭiṉapaḷaṭin ṭika cūnayīyaṭa - tuṭiracayēṭa ṭika vāna haḷayaivaiu ḷaṭiunaivala ṭika kṣanapēyaṭa; yaṭaḷaṭi “habṭicapaiinēḷa ṭika aiṉēyai.”
3.	“kūyavayacanayan ṭica ūhyaṭaṉanana”
4.	ḷucai ṟu. tuṭinēḷa (haḷayaivaiu cunaḷanayacauhnaḷa ēṭiṭa.i)
5.	aicūṉayain havēiḷūrayacala (hayīcaṭai 1872)
6.	āca. ḻucaṭiṟaḷana kṣa.ū.ḷa.
7.	ṭīḷavayaima
8.	ṭuyapērayapana ṭika vāna ṭuyayīyīḷa.
9.	kūhna tuṭiracayēṭa ṭika vāna ḻunacaayan ṭīcanaivēyaṭa ḷuṭiunaivala kaṭica 1869.
10.	“ha uṭiayīyacayavaiena ḻucayaaayaca ṭika vāna āṭiṉanacan cūṭin-hacalayan ṭuyapērayapana ṭika aiṉēyai” le tuṭihn kṣanayaanaḷa kṣa.ū.ḷu. 1872.
11.	ḻunaṭipacayayīhla.
12.	ēṭiṟanaṭa uhyapēnaḷa.
13.	“ḻucayaaayaca ṭika vāna ḷaiṉōi ṭuyapērayapana” - ṉuca. nucanēḷava kūcaraayīyī.

7. tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ enta iṉattaic cērntavai?
tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ cittiya moḻiyiṉattōṭu cērttaṟkuriyaṉavē yaṉṟi intu - airōppiya moḻiyiṉat tōṭu cērttaṟkuriyaṉavākā eṉpatu pērāciriyar rāsk1 eṉpāriṉ muṭipākum. cērttaṟkuriyaṉa eṉṟu maṭṭum kūṟalāmē yaṉṟi, cērntaṉa eṉṟu colliviṭutaṟkillai; avai cittiya moḻikaḷōṭu toṭarpuṭaiyaṉa vēyaṉṟic cittiya moḻiyiṉattiliruntu peṟappaṭṭavaiyalla. cittiya moḻiyīṉam eṉpatil piṉṉiṣ, turukki, maṅkōliyam, tuṅkūsiyam ākiyamoḻiyiṉaṅkaḷ aṭaṅkum. rāsk eṉpārē ivvāṟu mutaṉmutal vakaippaṭuttiṉār. im moḻikaḷ aṉaittum orē ilakkaṇa amaippuṭai yavaiyāy, orē paṭittāṉa potu vitikaḷaiyum piṉpaṟṟu kiṉṟaṉa. urupukaḷ allatu tuṇaic coṟkaḷaic cērppataṉālēyē avai ilakkaṇa vēṟupāṭuṟukiṉṟaṉa; cemittiya2 moḻiyiṉaṅ kaḷilō pakutiyiṉ uyirolikaḷai māṟṟuvatāl vēṟupāṭuṟu kiṉṟaṉa; cīṉattilum, piṟa taṉippaṭṭa ōracai moḻikaḷilum coṟṟoṭariluḷḷa coṟkaḷai muṉṉum piṉṉum māṟṟi yamaippatāl vēṟupaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivvakaiyil intu - airōppiya moḻikaḷum cittiya moḻikaḷaip pōlavē mutaṉmutal aṭukkiyal3 muṟaiyaiyē kaiyāṇṭiruntaṉa eṉṟu tōṟṟu kiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl avaṟṟiṉ aṭaikaḷ allatu tuṇaic coṟkaḷvaravarat tēyntu vikutikaḷākavō urupukaḷākavō māṟiviṭṭamaiyāl avai cittiya iṉaṅkaḷaip pōlavumiṉṟi, cīṉa moḻikaḷaip pōlavumiṉṟit taṉippaṭṭa oruvakai iṉamāyiṉa. cittiya moḻikaḷaic cilar tāttār moḻiyiṉam eṉpar; piṉṉiṣ iṉam, āltāyik iṉam, maṅkōliya iṉam, turāṉiya iṉam eṉṟum palavāṟākap palar kūṟuvar. āṉāl, ivaiyaṉaittum ipperu moḻik kuḻuviluḷḷa moḻi yiṉaṅkaḷ palavaṟṟuḷ oṉṟiraṇṭaiyē taṉittaṉikkuṟikkumāta lāl avaṟṟaip peru moḻik kuḻuviṉ peyarākak koḷḷal cālātu. itukāṟum, paṇṭaik kirēkka aṟiñarkaḷāl cittiyam eṉṟa peyar potuppaṭa āciyāvilum, airōppā vilum iruntuvanta nākarikamaṟṟa paṇṭai makkaḷaik kuṟikkavē payaṉpaṭuttap peṟṟuvantuḷḷamaiyāl ataṉaip peyarāka vaḻaṅkutalē cālpuṭaittām.
pēraṟiñar rāsk tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ cittiyac cārpuṭai yavai eṉak kūṟiṉavarkaḷuḷ mutalvarāyiṉum, ataṉait teḷivupaṭutti viḷakka ettakaiya muyaṟciyum avar ceyya villai. avaraip piṉpaṟṟiya palarum atēmātiri avar kūṟiyataik kūṟiṉarēyaṉṟi vēṟu oṉṟum ceyya villai. piriccārṭu4 eṉpār tam ārāyccikaḷil cittiya tirāviṭa moḻikaḷil cila potuppaṭaiyāṉa oṟṟumaikaḷai oṉṟiraṇṭu ilakkaṇac cārpāṉa eṭuttukkāṭṭukaḷuṭaṉ viḷakkip pōyiṉar. ātāraṅkaḷuṭaṉ āyntu viḷakkuvatu eḷitaṉṟu. ēṉeṉil aṅṅaṉam viḷakka vēṇṭumāyiṉ mutal mutalil tirāviṭa moḻikaḷait taṉittaṉi ārāyntu avaṟṟiṉ oppumaiyiṉmūlam avaṟṟiṉ paṇṭaiya urukkaḷaiyum ilakkaṇa amaitikaḷaiyum talaimaiyāṉa paṇpukaḷaiyum aṟivatōṭu, atēmātiri piṟa cittiya uṭpirivukaḷiṉ paṇpu kaḷaiyum uṇarntāka vēṇṭum. “ulaka varalāṟṟiṉ potup paṭaiyāṉa amaitikaḷ” eṉṟa tokutiyil māksmūlaratu “turāṉiyamoḻikaḷ paṟṟiya namatu iṉṟaiya aṟivunilai”5 eṉṟa ārāycciyurai piṉṉar veḷivantatu. an nūlmikac ciṟanta toṉṟu. eṉiṉum, annūlil āciriyar tām eṭuttukkoṇṭa poruḷ muḻuvataiyum, potuppaṭavē ārāyntuḷḷār. ataṉāl īṇṭu eṭuttukkoṇṭa poruḷukku atu putuc cāṉṟukaḷ oṉṟaiyum pakaravillai. tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiṉ ilakkaṇa amaitikaḷum, coṟṟoṭar muṟaiyum mēṟpōkkākap pārppavarkkukkūṭa viḷaṅkak kūṭumāyiṉum, avaṟṟiṉ olimuṟai, avaṟṟiṉ iṭavakai māṟṟaṅkaḷ, vaḻakkukaḷ, ilakkaṇa urumāṟṟaṅkaḷ mutaliyavai taṉippaṭṭa ārāycci yālaṉṟi viḷaṅkat takkavaiyalla.
iṉi, ivvakai ārāycciyait toṭaṅkumuṉ pōṭliṅ6 eṉpār varaiyaṟuttuk kūṟiya, piṉvarum karuttu niṉaivu kūraṟpāṟṟu: “tām naṉku ārāyntaṟiyāta moḻi kaḷaippaṟṟi oruvar eḻuta muṟpaṭutal tīṅku viḷaippatākum”. cittiya moḻiyaippaṟṟi ārāyavēṇṭumāṉāl, avaṟṟu ḷaṭaṅkiya palvēṟu moḻikaḷiṉ oppiyalkaḷum vēṟṟumai yiyalkaḷum naṉku terintiruttal vēṇṭuma. pālar7 eṉpār eḻutiya piṉṉiya moḻiyārāyccik kaṭṭuraikaḷum, pērāciriyar haṉ/palvi8 eṉpār eḻutiya turāṉiya moḻi yārāyccikaḷum ittuṟaiyil pērutavi puriya vallaṉavām. haṅkēriyaṉ, vōkul9 ostiyāk, piṉṉiṣ ākiya nāṉku moḻikaḷum orē potu moḻiyiṉattaic cōntavai eṉṟum, avaṟṟiṉ ilakkaṇaṅkaḷellām neruṅkiya toṭarpuṭaiyaṉa eṉṟum haṉ/palvi viḷakkiyuḷḷār.
pēraṟiñar pāṭ, piraṭerik mullar10 mutaliya pala moḻi yiyalār cittiya allatu turāṉiya moḻikaḷ orupotu moḻiyiliruntu vantavai eṉṟu uṟutiyākak kūṟamuṭiyātu eṉṟu karutukiṉṟaṉar. pēraṟiñar piḷākkum11 “turāṉiya moḻikaḷ palavum orupuṭai orē paṭittāṉa vaḷarcciyai aṭaintuḷḷa orē kuḻu moḻikaḷō allatu pala kuḻu moḻikaḷō ākum” eṉak karutukiṉṟaṉar. eṉiṉum potuppaṭa nōkka, kastrēṉ12 mutaliya aṟiñar kūṟukiṉṟapaṭi avaṟṟiṉ oṟṟumaikaḷ moḻikku iṉṟiyamaiyātavaiyākavum, palavākavum irukkiṟapaṭiyāl avaṟṟāl ēṟpaṭum toṭakkap potumaiyai, a/tāvatu orē mutaṉmoḻiyiṉiṉṟu avai toṭaṅki yirukkavēṇṭum eṉṟa muṭivai, ēṟkātirukka muṭiyātu. “im moḻikaḷiṉ col oṟṟumaikaḷum vaṭiva oṟṟumaikaḷum āriya cemittiya iṉaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum oṟṟumaiyiṉiṉṟum vēṟupaṭṭavai yāyiṉum, taṟceyalāy ēṟpaṭak kūṭiyavai alla, orē mutaṉmoḻiyiliruntu ēṟpaṭṭavaiyākavē irukkavēṇṭum eṉpatu teḷivu” eṉpar aṟiñar māksmūlarum, mēlum “neṭunāḷ pirintiyaṅkiya aṭukkiyal moḻikaḷuḷ, moḻiyiyaṟkaiyiṉ aṭippaṭaip pakutikaḷ allatu māṟupāṭu ceyyamuṭiyāta col vakaikaḷāṉa iṭappeyarkaḷ, eṇṇup peyarkaḷ, urupukaḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟait tavira vēṟu ettakaiya oṟṟumaikaḷaiyum nām etirpārkka muṭiyātu. mēṟpaṭaiyāṉa poruḷ koṇṭa coṟkaḷuḷ vēṟṟumai iruppataiviṭa, oṟṟumai yiruppatait tāṉ nām arumaiyāyk koḷḷa vēṇṭum” eṉṟum avar kūṟukiṟār.
mēlē tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ vaṭamoḻiyiṉiṉṟum vēṟupaṭṭavai eṉpataṟkuk kāṭṭiya ak kāraṇaṅkaḷaiyē avai cittiya iṉaṅkaḷuṭaṉ oṉṟupaṭṭavai eṉpataṟkum kāṭṭalākum. cittiya iṉattuṭpaṭṭa moḻikaḷ aṉaittum orē iṉam eṉpataiyum ak kāraṇaṅkaḷē kāṭṭum. avaṟṟiṉ viḷakkattaik kuṟainta aḷavil eṭuttuk koṇṭāluṅkūṭa avai toṭarpu uṭaiyavai eṉpatu aiyamaṟat teḷivākum. cila kūṟukaḷil maṭṭum tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ intu - airōppiya moḻikaḷai oṭṭiyavaiyāyk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa; a/tāvatu ōraḷavukku, “aṭukkiyal” moḻiyāy iruppatiliruntu muṉṉēṟi (intu-airōppiya moḻikaḷaip pōla)c “citaiviyal”13 moḻikaḷ ākiyuḷḷaṉa. vēṟṟumaiyilum viṉaittiripilum payaṉpaṭum tuṇaic coṟkaḷiṟ pala taṉi moḻiyāka vaḻakkāṟaṟṟuppōyiṉa. āṉāl, i/toṉṟai muṉṉiṭṭu, tirāviṭa moḻikaḷaic cittiya iṉattiliruntu pirikka vēṇṭiyatillai; ēṉeṉil it tuṇaiccoṟkaḷ ciṟcila iṭaṅkaḷil muṟṟilum urupukaḷ allatu vikutikaḷākak kuṟaintuviṭṭaṉavāyiṉum, coṟkaḷiṉ pakutikaḷiliruntu ivai eppoḻutum pirikkappaṭak kūṭiyaṉavākavē iruntu varukiṉṟaṉa; intu-airōppiya moḻikaḷiṟ pōṉṟu ivai pakutiyuṭaṉ oṉṟupaṭṭu orē moḻiyiṉ kūṟāyviṭavillai. intu - airōppiya moḻikaḷil ivai aṅṅaṉam kalantu viṭṭapaṭiyāl pakuti etu, vikuti etu eṉṟu pirippatu kūṭa aritāy viṭukiṟatu. ataṉōṭu, turukkiyam, piṉṉiyam, haṅkēriyam, jappāṉiyam mutaliya moḻikaḷ aṉaittum tirāviṭa moḻiyaiviṭac “citaiviyal” tuṟaiyil mēmpaṭṭiruppiṉum, akkāraṇattāl cittiya iṉattiliruntu vilakkappaṭavillai eṉpataiyum ōrtal vēṇṭum.
“moḻiyiyalil cīṉattiṉ iṭam” eṉṟa nūlil eṭkiṉs cittiya iṉattiṉ orumai, avaṟṟuṭaṉ tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiṉ toṭarpu ākiya koḷkaikaḷaip peritum ēṟṟuk koḷḷukiṉṟār. avar kāṭṭum pala taṟceyalāṉa, nuṇukka māṉa oṟṟumaikaḷ āḻntu ārāyumaḷavil nilainiṟkā. āyiṉum potuppaṭak kūṟumaḷavil ittuṟaiyil oruvar ārāyuntōṟum tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ maṅkōliya moḻikaḷ ākiya iraṇṭum oruvāṟu orumaippāṭuṭaiyaṉa eṉpataik kāṇpar. tirāviṭa cīṉa moḻikaḷiṭai oṟṟumaikaḷ mikuti eṉak kūṟamuṭiyātu. eṭkiṉs “cittiya iṉammaṭṭumaṉṟu, āciya airōppiya moḻikaḷ aṉaittumē orē mutaṉmoḻiyiṉ vakait tiripukaḷ ākum” eṉkiṟār. ituvarai maṉita vakuppiṉ moḻikaḷai aṟiñar ārāynta aḷavil ikkōṭpāṭu poruttamākat tōṟṟiṉum aṟiviyal muṟaippaṭi itu muṟṟilum nilaināṭṭappaṭa iṉṉum neṭunāḷ cellak kūṭum. kaṭaiciyāka, tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiṉ amaippukaḷai viḷakkac cittiya moḻiyiṉattai oṟṟumaippaṭutti ārāynta aṟivu evvaḷavō payaṉpaṭukiṟatu eṉṟa oru ceytiyē avaṟṟuḷ cittiyat toṭarpu irukkiṟatu eṉpataṟkōr aṟikuṟi yākum. itaṟku eṭuttukkāṭṭākat tirāviṭa moḻiyiluḷḷa viṉaiccoṟkaḷiṉ peyareccattiṉ amaippai eṭuttuk koḷḷalām. maṅkōliyam, mañcu ivaṟṟil i/tu eṅṅaṉam amaintuḷḷatu eṉpatai aṟinta piṉṉarē, tirāviṭa moḻikaḷil eṅṅaṉam amaintuḷḷatu eṉpatai eḷitil aṟiyamuṭintatu. aṅṅaṉam aṟinta viḷakkattai ituvarai yārum maṟukkavō allatu vēṟu vakaiyil viḷakkavō illai eṉpatu kuṟipiṭattakkatu.
cittiya iṉaccārpuk koḷkaiyai uṟutippaṭuttum vakaiyil maṟṟoru cāṉṟuṅ kiṭaittuḷḷatu. atuvē mēṟku muṭiyā14 allatu pārasīkattil uḷḷa pehistaṉ allatu pakistāṉ aṭṭavaṇaikaḷiṉ moḻipeyarppukkaḷ ākum. iv aṭṭavaṇaikaḷ ṭaraiyas hisṭāspas15 eṉpavar tammaippaṟṟit tāmē eḻutiya varalāṟu ākum. ivai paḻam pārasīka moḻiyilum, pāpilōṉiya moḻiyilum, pārasīka, mīṭiyap pēraraciluḷḷa cittiya makkaḷum aṟiyum paṭiyāka avarkaḷatu tāymoḻiyilum eḻutappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟiṉ moḻipeyarppu tirāviṭa cittiya uṟavai viḷakkum vakaiyil oru puttoḷiyāy viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. ivaṟṟiṉ iraṇṭām tokuti teḷivākac cittiyac cārpāṉatu eṉṟu nāris16 tamatu kaṭṭuraiyil eḻutukiṟār.17 pēraṟiñar āpparṭ18 im moḻiyaip pēciya makkaḷ mīṭiyarē eṉṟu kūṟukiṟār. āyiṉum avarum im moḻi cittiya allatu turāṉiyac cārpāṉateṉṟē koḷkiṟār. itaṉāl, ki.mu. 5ām nūṟṟāṇṭilēyē pēcappaṭṭa oru cittiya iṉa moḻiyuṭaṉ tirāviṭa moḻikaḷai oṟṟumaippaṭuttum ōr ariya vāyppuk kiṭaittuḷḷatu. immoḻi potuppaṭac cittiya iṉac cārpuṭaiyatē eṉiṉum, avaṟṟuṭ ciṟappāka ukrō - piṉṉiṣ kuḻuvuṭaṉ iṉṉum neruṅkiya toṭarpuṭaiya tākavuṅ kāṭṭappaṭukiṟatu. tirāviṭamoḻikaḷ inta ukrō - piṉṉiṣ moḻikaḷaic ciṟappāka ottirukkiṉṟaṉa eṉpatu iṅkē kūṟavēṇṭiya toṉṟākum. 
tirāviṭa moḻikaḷukkum, ip pehistaṉ aṭṭa vaṇaiyiṉ moḻikkum iṭaiyē kāṇappaṭum oṟṟumaikaḷ varumāṟu:
(1)  iv aṭṭavaṇaikaḷil ṭ ṭa, ṭa, ṇa eṉṟa nā aṭi aṇṇa olikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. itē olikaḷ tirāviṭa moḻikaḷilum uḷḷaṉa. vaṭamoḻiyilum ivai uḷḷaṉa vāyiṉum (piṟa āriya moḻikaḷuḷ illāmaiyai nōkkat) tirāviṭa moḻikaḷaip piṉpaṟṟi vaṭamoḻiyil ēṟpaṭṭa olikaḷē ivai eṉa ūkikkalām. nārisum ik karuttaiyē veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷār.
(2) tirāviṭa moḻikaḷuḷ tamiḻaippōla, iv aṭṭavaṇai moḻikaḷuḷ valleḻuttukkaḷ moḻimutalil varumpoḻutu iraṭṭitta iṭattum valloli uṭaiyavai yākavum, moḻi iṭaiyē uyirkaḷiṉ naṭuvē varum poḻutu aṇmai allatu arai aṅkāppu oliyuṭaiyavākavum olikkiṉṟaṉa.
(3) ivvaṭṭavaṇai moḻiyiṉ āṟām vēṟṟumai urupukaḷ ṉa, ṉiṉa, iṉṉa eṉpavaiyākum. ivaṟṟiṟkuc cariyāṉa tirāviṭa urupukaḷ teluṅku ṉī eṉpatum, kōṇṭu allatu pirākuvi ṉa a eṉpavaiyum, tamiḻ iṉ eṉpatum ākum.
(4) aṭṭavaṇaikaḷil nāṉkām vēṟṟumai urupu ikki allatu ikka eṉpatākum. itaṉai yotta urupukaḷ tāttār - turukkiyak kuḻuvilum, ukriyaṉ iṉattilum uḷa. āṉāl itaṉai muṟṟilum ottiruppavai tirāviṭa urupukaḷākiya ku, ki, ka eṉpavaiyām. tamiḻ, malaiyāḷa moḻikaḷil ivvurupukaḷ iraṭṭikkumpōtu avaṟṟiṉ muṉ u allatu i collolic cāriyaiyāka varukiṉṟatu. aṭṭavaṇaikaḷil uṉakku eṉṉum poruḷil niṉikka (niṉ-ikka) eṉṉum colluṭaṉ kaṉṉaṭam niṉake (niṉ-a-ke) malaiyāḷam niṉakku (niṉ=a-k) eṉpavaṟṟai oppiṭṭu nōkkuka.
(5) aṭṭavaṇaikaḷiluḷḷa iṭappeyarkaḷiṉ iraṇṭām vēṟṟumai urupu uṉ, iṉ, ṉ eṉpavaiyām. ittuṭaṉ teluṅku uruvu ṉu, ṉi eṉpataiyum kaṉṉaṭam am, aṉṉu (aṉṉ- u) eṉpataiyum oppiṭṭu nōkkuka.
(6) aṭṭavaṇaikaḷil varum eṇṇup peyarkaḷil eḻuttil eḻutiya peyar oṉṟu eṉṉum poruḷil varum kir eṉṟa col maṭṭumēyām. i/tu uṇmaiyil peyaruriccol vaṭivattilēyē (a/tāvatu paṇput tokaiyākavē) eḻutappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum. teluṅkil ivvaṭivam oka- eṉpatum, tamiḻil ōr -eṉpatum ākum. ku-moḻiyil tamiḻ oru eṉpataṟkuc cariyāṉa col ra eṉpatām. ik ku moḻiyiṉ vaṭivattiṟku aṭṭavaṇaiyiluḷḷa piṟitoru vaṭivamākiya ra allatu irra mikavum nerukkiyatākum.
aṭṭavaṇaiyil eṇṇup peyarkaḷ peyaraṭaiyākum poḻutu im eṉṟa cāriyaik koḷḷukiṉṟaṉa. itu tamiḻ ām eṉpataiyum sāmōyiyat im eṉpataiyum niṉaivūṭṭu kiṉṟatu.
(7) tirāviṭa moḻikaḷilum, aṭṭavaṇaiyilum muṉṉilaip peyar oṉṟē. ivai iraṇṭilum eḻuvāy nī; piṟavēṟṟamaikku muṉṉaiya mutal vaṭivu niṉ eṉpatākum. tamakkuk kīḻppaṭiyiluḷḷavarkaḷai aḻaikka muṉṉilai orumaiyaiyē vaḻaṅkivanta kāraṇattāl ivvaṭṭa vaṇaikaḷil muṉṉilaippaṉmai kāṇappaṭavillai.
(8) aṭṭavaṇai moḻiyilum tirāviṭa moḻikaḷaip pōlavē peyareccam irukkiṟatu. āṉāl atōṭu kūṭavē atil iṇaippu iṭappeyar19 oṉṟum kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. āṉāl ippakuti pāracīkattiṉiṉṟum moḻipeyarkkap paṭṭatātalāl pāracīkattiluḷḷa iṇaippu iṭappeyarait tāṉ iṅkē puṉaintu uṇṭupaṇṇi yirukkavēṇṭum eṉṟu nāris karutukiṟār. aṭṭavaṇaiyiṉ peyareccam uruvil tirāviṭap peyareccattiṉiṉṟum vēṟupaṭṭiruppiṉum ataṉ poruḷum payaṉum tirāviṭa vaḻakkai ottē irukkiṉṟaṉa. ippeyareccamē cittiya iṉac cārpuṭaiya moḻikaḷ aṉaittiṟkum potuvāṉa ciṟappup paṇpu eṉak koḷḷalām pōlum!
(9) aṭṭavaṇaiyiṉ ēval etirmaṟai muṭipu iṉṉi kōṇṭil itu miṉṉi ākum.
ivvoṟṟumaikaḷ nīṅkalākap piṟavēṟṟumait tiripukaḷil pehistaṉ aṭṭavaṇaiyiṉ moḻi haṅkēriyam, mōrṭuviṉ20 mutaliya piṟa ukriya moḻikaḷai ottirukkiṉṟatu. āṉāl, ivvakaikaḷilellām tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ vēṟupaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ viṉaiccollākkam cikkalaṟṟatu. avai perumpālum kālaṅkāṭṭum iṭainilaikaḷuṭaṉum, avaṟṟiṉpiṉ, pāl, iṭam kāṭṭa avaṟṟiṟkuriya iṭappeyar kaḷiṉ tiripukaḷaiyē koṇṭum viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. ivvakaiyil vēṟṟumaikaḷ iruntumkūṭa, mēṟkūṟiya oṟṟumaikaḷ iṉṟiyamaiyāc ciṟappuṭaiyaṉa ātaliṉ, tirāviṭa moḻi kaḷukkum cittiya moḻikaḷukkum iṭaiyiluḷḷa uṟavu oruciṟitēyāyiṉum a/tu aṭippaṭaiyāṉatē eṉpatil aiyamirukka muṭiyātu. ēṉeṉil, ivvaḷavu vēṟṟumai kaḷukkiṭaiyilum iṉṟiyamaiyā oṟṟumaikaḷum kāṇak kiṭakkiṉṟaṉavātaliṉ. ivvoṟṟumaikaḷiliruntu peṟap paṭuvaṉa yāvai yeṉil, tirāviṭar varalāṟṟuk kālaṅkaḷukku neṭunāḷ muṉṉarē innāṭṭilēyē iruntavarkaḷ eṉpatum, avarkaḷum ulaka makkaṭ kuḻuviṉar aṉaivarukkum mutaṟ piṟappiṭamākak karutappaṭum naṭu āciyāviṉiṉṟē iṅku vantavarkaḷ eṉpatumēyām. mutalil āriyariṭamiruntum, piṉ ukrō- turāṉiyariṭamiruntum pirintu ivarkaḷ intiyāvai nōkki vantirukkavēṇṭum eṉpatum, varum vaḻiyil palūccistāṉattil oru pakutiyiṉaraik kuṭiyēṟaviṭṭu vantirukkavēṇṭum eṉpatum eḷitil ūkikkappaṭum.
iṅṅaṉam ilakkaṇa amaippu mutaliya pala oṟṟumaip paṇpukaḷiṉ mūlam tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ cittiyac cārpuṭaiyavai eṉṟu koḷḷappaṭiṉum, avai intu -  airōppiya moḻiyiṉac cārpum uṭaiyavaiyē eṉpatu viyakkattakka cila oppumaikaḷāl uyttuṇarappaṭum. avaṟṟiṉ cittiyat toṭarpukūṭat taṉippaṭat turukkiyak kuḻuvuṭaṉō, ukriya, maṅkōliya, tuṅkūsiya moḻikaḷuṭaṉō aṉṟu; am moḻikaḷ aṉaittum tammuḷ evvaḷavō vēṟṟumai yuṭaiyaṉa; āyiṉum avai yaṉaittiṟkum, tāyāyuḷḷa oru pēriṉa moḻiyuṭaṉēyē tirāviṭa moḻiyiṉaṅkaḷ toṭarpuṭaiyavaiyām. ivaṟṟuḷ tirāviṭattiṟku mikavum aṇmaiyuṭaiyateṉak koḷḷakkūṭiyatu piṉṉiṣ allatu ukriyamē. ostiyakkuṭaṉum cila ciṟappāṉa uṟavukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. ilakkaṇa oppumai, col tokuti oppumai ākiyavaṟṟiṉ mūlamākaṭṭum ārāyntu kaṇṭa im muṭipu pehistaṉ aṭṭavaṇaikaḷiṉ veḷiyīṭṭāl peritum uṟutiyaṭaivatu kāṇka. itaṉāl mīṭō - pāracīkaratu paṭai yeṭuppiṟku muṉ naṭu āciyāvil kuṭiyirunta makkaḷ turukkiya allatu maṅkōliyak kuḻuviṉar allar, ukriyak kuḻuviṉarē eṉpatu viḷaṅkum. veppa maṇṭalattuḷ irukkum tirāviṭak kuḻu eṅkē, vaṭa turuvap pakkattilirukkum piṉṉiyar, ostiyakkar, ukriyar eṅkē!ivarkaḷiṭaiyē ettakaiya toṭarpu?
tirāviṭa moḻikaḷaic cittiyac cārpuṭaiyavai eṉṟu kaṇittaṟkāṉa teḷivukaḷai ārāykaiyil niṉaivil vaikka vēṇṭiya toṉṟuṇṭu. a/tāvatu, orupuṟam cittiya iṉa moḻikaḷiṉ aṭippaṭaiyāṉa “tokka iyal” paṇpukaḷ teḷivāṉa vuruvuṭaiyavai āka iruppiṉum, iṉṉorupuṟam col tokutiyilum, vēṟu pala ciṟu kuṟippukaḷilum oṉṟukkoṉṟu malaikkum maṭuvukkum uḷḷa vēṟṟumai uṭaiyavaiyā yirukkiṉṟaṉa eṉpatē. uṇmaiyil, intu - airōppiya iṉa moḻikaḷ oṉṟukku oṉṟu vēṟṟumaip paṭuvataiviṭa, cittiya moḻikaḷ oṉṟukkoṉṟu ettaṉaiyō maṭaṅku mikunta vēṟṟumaiyuṭaiyaṉa eṉṟu kūṟutal vēṇṭum. eṭuttuk kāṭṭāka, intu - airōppiya moḻikaḷ aṉaittilum eṇkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ ottirukkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu coṉṉāṟ pōtātu, (vaṭamoḻi oṉṟu eṉṉum poruḷ koṇṭa ēka eṉpatu nīṅkalāka maṟṟavai aṉaittum) orē vaṭivuṭaiyavai yākavē irukkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟuṅ kūṟutal vēṇṭum. itaṟku nērmāṟāka, cittiya iṉa moḻikaḷil aṉaittiṟkum potuvāṉa oṟṟumaikaḷ arukiyirukkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu coṉṉāṟ pōtātu; ētēṉum iraṇṭu moḻikaḷukkup potuvāṉa eṇkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷkūṭa mika māṟupaṭṭē yirukkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟuṅ kūṟutal vēṇṭum. iv vēṟṟumaikaḷiṉ mikutippāṭṭai nōkkiṉ, intac cittiya iṉam intu - airōppiya iṉattaip pōl oru taṉi iṉam eṉṟu colvataiviṭap pala iṉaṅkaḷai uṭkoṇṭatōr pēriṉam eṉṟu colvatē cālum. ippēriṉattuṭpaṭṭa kuḻukkaḷiṉ orumaippāṭu nuṇukkamāṉa oṟṟumaikaḷāl niṟuvakkūṭiya taṉṟu, perumpōkkāṉa vaṭivu allatu amaippu oṟṟumai kaḷiṉālēyē niṟuvappaṭum. eṉavē, intu airōppiya iṉattuṭpaṭṭa moḻikaḷākiya vaṭamoḻi, sentu (kīḻap paḻam pārasīkam), paḻampārasīkam, kirēkkam, ilattīṉam, kātiklittu vēṉiyam, slavōṉiyam, kelttiyam mutaliyavai ovvoṉṟum ōriṉattuṭpaṭṭa taṉi moḻikaḷēyām. āṉāl, cittiyap pēriṉattilē ivaṟṟilum mikunta vēṟṟumaikaḷai yuṭaiya ciṟṟiṉa moḻikaḷ aintāṟukku mēṟpaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivvuṭpiriviṉaṅkaḷ ovvoṉṟilum kiṭṭattaṭṭa intu - airōppiya moḻikaḷil ettaṉait taṉi moḻikaḷ uṇṭō attaṉait taṉi moḻikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. ivaipōka vakaippaṭutti innāḷvarai iṉamākac cērkka muṭiyāta nilaiyuṭaiya irupatu muppatu taṉi moḻikaḷkūṭa uḷḷaṉa.
intu - airōppiya iṉattiṟkum cittiyap pēriṉattiṟkum iṭaiyil uḷḷa ipperu vēṟṟumaikkuk kāraṇam mutaṉmutaṟ koṇṭē muṉṉaiya iṉattavar aṟivum nākarikamum paṭaittiruntamaiyum, avarkaḷ moḻikaḷ iṉṉum muntiya nāḷ mutaṟkoṇṭē tirutta muṟṟu mutaṟcoṟkaḷum amaitikaḷum citaiyāmal pātukākkappaṭṭu vantuḷḷamaiyum tāṉ. cittiya iṉattavar nāṭōṭikaḷāyt tiripavarā yiruntamaiyum, āriyar oḻuṅkāṉa kuṭivāḻkkai uṭaiyavarā yiruntamaiyum itaṟku aṭippaṭait tūṇṭutalkaḷā yiruntirukka vēṇṭum. kāraṇam etuvāyiṉum cari, cittiya moḻikaḷiṉ iṉa uṟavukaḷai ārāyum vakaiyil icceytiyai niṉaivil vaittuk koḷḷal vēṇṭum. neṭunāḷ pirikkappaṭṭa cittiya iṉa moḻikaḷiṭaiyē atē iyalpuṭaiya intu - airōppiya moḻikaḷil kāṇappaṭum nuṇukkamāṉa oṟṟumaikaḷai etirpārkka muṭiyātu. tiruntā moḻikaḷiṭaiyē, oru moḻiyiṉ vakaittiripukaḷiṭaik kūṭa mika viraivāṉa māṟutalkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭu, māṟupāṭum mikutiyāyviṭṭataṟkāṉa eṭuttuk kāṭṭukaḷaip pēraṟiñar māksmūlar tamatu “moḻiyiyal coṟpoḻivukaḷil” kuṟippiṭukiṟār. itē māksmūlarukku varainta kaṭitamoṉṟil pāṭṭisaṉ pātiriyārum “oṉṟaṉai yoṉṟu aṭuttuḷḷa tīvukaḷiraṇṭiṉ moḻikaḷ col tokutiyil muṟṟilum vēṟupaṭṭum, amaippu oṉṟil maṭṭumē oṉṟupaṭṭum kāṇappaṭuvatu iyalpu” eṉṟu itaṉōṭoppak kūṟutal kāṇka.
oru taṉimoḻikku oru moḻiyiṉattuṭaṉ mūṉṟu vakaiyāṉa uṟavumuṟai irukka muṭiyum. (1) nērāṉa kālvaḻi muṟai (2) uṭaṉiyaṅkun tuṇaimoḻi muṟai (3) avviṉattuṭ cārum cārpumuṟai. ivaṟṟuḷ mūṉṟām uṟavu muṟaiyaiyē tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ cittiya moḻiyiṉattuṭaṉ koṇṭuḷḷaṉavākak kūṟalām. eṉiṉum, tirāviṭa moḻiyārāycciyāḷarkaḷ itaṉai oppukkoṇṭatākak kūṟa muṭiyātu. tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ peritum intu - airōppiya iṉattaic cērntavaiyē eṉṟu tirāviṭa moḻiyārāyccip pēraṟiñar ṭākṭar pōp karutukiṟār. “tamiḻk kaic cuvaṭi”21 eṉṉum tam nūliṉ muṉṉuraiyil avar piṉvarumāṟu eḻutiyuḷḷār: “teṉṉintiya moḻikaḷai evvaḷavukkevvaḷavu āḻntu paṭikkiṉṟōmō avvaḷavukkavvaḷavu avai vaṭamoḻit toṭarpuṭaiyaṉaveṉavum, intu - airōppiya moḻiyiṉat tuṭaṉ mikavum neruṅkiya paṇṭait toṭarpu uṭaiyaṉa veṉavum kāṇalām. āṉāl, avai veṟum pākataṅkaḷ eṉṟō, vaṭamoḻic citaivukaḷ eṉṟō kūṟiviṭa muṭiyātu. avai intu - airōppiya moḻiyiṉattuḷ iṭam peṟa vēṇṭiyaṉavē eṉṟum, vaṭamoḻikku iṇaiyāṉa vēṟoru tuṇaimoḻiyiṉ citainta kūṟukaḷē avai eṉṟum, avaiyum vaṭamoḻiyum orē paṇṭai mūla moḻiyiṉ kiḷaikaḷ eṉṟum yām eppoḻutum karuti vantuḷōm. intu airōppiya moḻiyiṉattaic cērnta kirēkkam, kātik22, persiyaṉ mutaliya moḻikaḷuṭaṉ avai neruṅkiya toṭarpuṭaiyaṉa eṉpataṟku uṟatiyāṉa cāṉṟukaḷ pala uḷa; attakaiya toṭarpu vaṭamoḻikku iruntatākac colvataṟ killai”. “malaic coṟpoḻivu”23 eṉum viviliyap pakutiyait tirāviṭamoḻikaḷ nāṉkilum moḻipeyarttut tantuḷḷa tam nūliṉ muṉṉuraiyil, pōp piṉvarumāṟu eḻutiyuḷḷār. “intat tirāviṭa moḻikaḷukkum keltik24 teyuttōṉiyam25 ākiya iru moḻikaḷukkum uḷḷa aṭippaṭaiyāṉa āḻnta uṟavukaḷai moḻiyiyalār kūrntu nōkkutalvēṇṭum. iṭamum kālamum vāyppiṉ iv voppumaikaḷai viritteḻutak kūṭum. in nūliṉ aṭutta patippilō vēṟu nūlilō itu paṟṟi mēl eḻutak karutiyuḷḷōm. muḻu oppumai akara varicai oṉṟālaṉṟi vēṟetaṉālum im moḻikaḷiṉ iṉa uṟavai viḷakkutalaritu.” kaṭaiciyākap pōp tamatu “tutamoḻi ilakkaṇac curukkat”26 tiṉ muṉṉuraiyil piṉvarumāṟu kūṟukiṟār: “pēraṟiñar kālṭuveluṭaṉ yām peritum otta karuttuṭaiyōm āyiṉum, tirāviṭa moḻikaḷukkum, kelttiya moḻikaḷukkum iṭaiyēyuḷḷa uṟavu iṉṉum naṉku ārāyak kiṭappatoṉṟeṉa eṇṇukiṉṟōm” eṉpatē. ittuṟai ārāycci mikavum iṉṟiyamaiyātatē eṉpatil aiyamillai. pōp intu - airōppiya moḻiyuṭaṉē oṟṟumai, uṟavu, iṉa oṟṟumai eṉṟu kūṟuvaṉa vellām emakku uṭaṉpāṭē. in nūliṉ mutaṟpatippil avaṟṟai yāmē eṭuttukkāṭṭiyum uḷḷōm. āṉāl av eṭuttukkāṭṭukkaḷiliruntu pōp koṇṭa muṭipu em muṭīvāṉa muṭipuṭaṉ vēṟupaṭukiṟatu. ematu kōṭpāṭu cittiya, intu- airōppiya uṟavukaḷ iraṇṭaṟkum iṭan tarupavaiyāka iruppiṉum, intu - airōppiya iṉattaiviṭac cittiya iṉamē tirāviṭak kuḻuvōṭu neruṅkiya uyir nilaiyāṉa toṭarpu uṭaiyatu eṉpatākum. kelttiya uṟavukaḷaippaṟṟi eṭuttukkoṇṭāl, intu - airōppiya moḻikaḷ aṉaittilum immoḻiyiṉamē potuppaṭac cittiya iṉattuṭaṉum, ciṟappākap piṉṉiyak kuḻuvuṭaṉum mika neruṅkiya toṭarpuṭaiyavaiyākat tōṉṟukiṟatu. kelttiyar airōppāvukku varumpōtu aṅkirunta muṉ kuṭikaḷ kelttik moḻiyaiyum piṉṉiṣ moḻiyaiyum pēci vantārkaḷō eṉpatu ārāyattakkatu. mēlum, kelttiya uṟavukaḷ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum uṟavukaḷ ciṟappākak kelttiya uṟavukaḷ tāṉā allatu uṇmaiyil cittiya uṟavukaḷ tāṉā eṉpatum oppiṭṭu ārāyat takkatoṉṟām.
inta ārāycciyiṉ toṭakkattilēyē mēṟkuṟippiṭṭa tirāviṭa-intu=airōppiya oṟṟumaikaḷ oppuk koḷḷap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. ataṟkākak kuṟittuḷḷa eṭuttukkāṭṭukaḷil cila oṟṟumaikaḷ aṭippaṭaiyāṉavai alla; ātalāl āḻntu nōkkum aḷavil maṟaintuviṭukiṉṟaṉa. (tamiḻ oṉṟu allatu oṇṇu; ilattīṉam ūṉus; tamiḻ añcu; vaṭamoḻi pañca(ṉ); tamiḻ eṭṭu; vaṭamoḻi aṣṭa(ṉ) eṉpaṉa pōlpavai). ivaṟṟaip puṟakkaṇittu viṭṭup pārttāluṅ kūṭat tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiṉ ilakkaṇa amaitikaḷiṟ palavum, mutaṟ coṟkaḷiṟ palavum intu-airōppiya moḻikaḷuṭaṉ oppumaiyuṭaiyavaiyāy irukkiṉṟaṉa. āyiṉum, uyir nilaiyāṉa amaippu muḻuvatum aiyamaṟac cittiyac cārpuṭaiya tātalāl cittiya iṉa uṟavē valiyuṟuttappaṭṭatu epirēya mutuc coṟkaḷiṟ pala vaṭamoḻi mutaṟcoṟkaḷuṭaṉ toṭarpuṭaiyavai eṉṟu kāṭṭappaṭukiṉṟamai karuti, epirēya moḻiyaic cemittiya iṉattiṉiṉṟum pirittu intu - airōppiya moḻiyākak kaṇikkātatupōla, vaṭamoḻi, kirēkkam, kātik, kelttik, pārasīkam, mutaliya pala moḻikaḷuṭaṉ viyakkat takum oṟṟumaikaḷ tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭiṉum tirāviṭa moḻikaḷaic cittiya iṉattiliruntum pirikka vēṇṭiyatillai. “ciṉikō = āryaka” eṉṟa nūlil27 pēraṟiñar ṣlejel āriya cīṉa oppumaikaḷaic cuṭṭikkāṭṭi āriya cīṉa moḻikaḷ tammuḷ iṉa uṟavuṭaiyaṉa eṉṟu viḷakka muyalkiṟār. avar kūṟiya uṟavu uṟutippaṭṭu viṭiṉuṅ kūṭac cīṉam āriyamoḻi eṉṟu āyviṭamāṭṭātu. iru moḻikaḷilum mikap paḻamaiyāṉa cila mutaṟ coṟkaḷ uḷḷaṉa eṉṟutāṉ muṭivu ceyyappaṭum. mēlum cīṉamoḻi maṭṭumēyaṉṟi, vaṭamoḻiyum, epirēyamuṅkūṭa mutaṉmutalil ōracai moḻikaḷāy iruntaṉa eṉṟu ārāycciyāl teriyavarukiṉṟatu. perumpālum tirāviṭa mutaṟcoṟkaḷum aṅṅaṉam ōracaic coṟkaḷē eṉpatu ivvārāycciyāl pulaṉākum. ivvakaiyil pēraṟiñar pḷīk28 eṉpār āriya moḻikaḷ mikap paḻaimaiyāṉatoru kālattil, “tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiṉ tākkutalkaḷukku uṭpaṭṭirukkavēṇṭum” eṉak koḷkiṟār. avar kūṟuvatāvatu:  “pāl vakuppuṭaiya moḻikaḷuḷ ip potuppāl tiruntiya muṟaiyil vaḻaṅkuvatu āṅkilam oṉṟilētāṉ. āṉāl paḻaiya moḻikaḷuḷ āṅkilattiṟku oppat tiruttamāka itaṉait tirāviṭa moḻik kuḻuvē vaḻaṅki vantuḷḷatu. āṅkilamum tirāviṭa moḻikaḷum ip pālai vaḻaṅkum iṭaṅkaḷ, ellai ākiyavaṟṟuḷ muṟṟilum ottirukkiṉṟaṉa. (āṇ, peṇ, potu eṉṟa) muppāl pākupāṭṭai uṭaiya kuḻukkaḷ ivai iraṇṭumē eṉpatai nōkka ivai uṟavaṟṟavai eṉṟu koḷḷutal iyal pākat tōṟṟavillai. pāl vakuppaiyuṭaiya āriya iṉattuṭaṉ tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ itukāṟum toṭarpupaṭuttappaṭa villaiyāyiṉum, āriya moḻikaḷ uru aṭaiyāmal vaḷarntu vanta kālattil avaṟṟil tirāviṭattākku ēṟpaṭṭataṉālēyē ivvoṟṟumaiyum piṟa oṟṟumaikaḷum ēṟpaṭṭirukkalām eṉṟu kūṟa iṭamuṇṭu” eṉpatē. ārāycci eṭṭumaḷavum pārttālum mikap paḻamaiyāṉa kālattilkūṭat tirāviṭa moḻikaḷil ip potuppāl orumaip (oṉṟaṉpāl) peyar iruntatākak kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. āṉāl viṉaiccollil pāl pākupāṭu ēṟpaṭṭatu piṟkālattilē eṉṟutāṉ eṇṇa vēṇṭum. intiyāvukkuḷ tirāviṭar varuṅkāl avarkaḷ moḻiyiṉ viṉaiccollil vikutiyē iruntirukka iṭamillai; eṉavē, pāl pākupāṭum iruntirukka muṭiyātu. ulakil vēṟenta moḻikaḷaiyumviṭat tirāviṭa moḻi viṉaiccoṟkaḷilēyē pāl pākupāṭu niṟaivuṭaiyatāka irukkiṉṟatu eṉpatu piṉ teḷivuṟuttappaṭum. ittakaiya pāl pākupāṭṭu vaḷarcci vaṭamoḻic cārpiṉāl uṇṭāṉa toṉṟākātu; ataṟku nērmāṟākavē uṇṭāyiruttal vēṇṭum.
tirāviṭa moḻikaḷil vaṭamoḻiyuṭaṉum intu - airōppiya moḻikaḷuṭaṉum toṭarpuṭaiya cila vērc coṟkaḷum, col vaṭivaṅkaḷum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu potuppaṭa mēṟkūṟappaṭṭatu. tavaṟāṉa cila karuttu muṭipukaḷ ak kūṟṟiliruntu eḻakkūṭumātaliṉ, avaṟṟai vilakkutal iṉṟiyamaiyātatākum. āriyarkaḷum tirāviṭarkaḷum pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷ kalantu uṟaintu vantamaiyāl, vaṭamoḻi yiliruntu tiraviṭamoḻikaḷ pala  coṟkaḷaik koṇṭu mēṟkūṟiya moḻiyoppumaik karuttai īṇṭu yām viḷakka muyalavillai. iru moḻikaḷiṉ aṭippaṭaiyāṉa mūlac coṟkaḷilum, collamaippilum kāṇappaṭum oṟṟumaikaḷē yām eṭuttuk koṇṭaṉavākum. tammaiyaṭuttu vaḻaṅkivanta vaḷamikka piṟamoḻikaḷiliruntu tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ pala coṟkaḷaik kaṭaṉ vāṅkiyuḷḷaṉa eṉpatu oppukkoḷḷat takkatē; āṉāl atupōlavē vaṭamoḻiyum ciṟcila vakaikaḷil tirāviṭa moḻic coṟkaḷaik kaṭaṉ vāṅkip payaṉpaṭuttap piṉ vāṅkavillai eṉpataiyum oppuk koḷḷutal vēṇṭuma. potuvāka oru moḻiyiliruntu maṟṟoru moḻiyil ivvāṟu vantēṟiyuḷḷa coṟkaḷaip pirittaṟital aritaṉṟu. eṭuttukkoṇṭa pala coṟkaḷum, collamaippukaḷum vaṭamoḻiyōṭumaṭṭumaṉṟi intu-airōppiya moḻiyiṉattuṭaṉum oṟṟumaiyuṭaiyaṉavākak kāṇap peṟukiṉṟaṉa. iṉṉuṅ kūṟappōṉāl vaṭamoḻiyiṟ kāṇappeṟā oṟṟumaikaḷ kirēkkattilum ilattīṉilum kāṇappeṟukiṉṟaṉa. vaṭamoḻiyil kāṇappeṟum oṟṟumai kaḷum ciṟcila iṭaṅkaḷil, tiruntiya amaippuṭaiya piṟkāla vaṭamoḻiyilaṉṟip (smskrutam) paṇṭaiya tiruntāta vaṭamoḻiyilēyēyākum.
tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiṟ kāṇappeṟum ittakaiya koṭukkal vāṅkal cārpaṟṟa aṭippaṭaiyāṉa oṟṟumaikaḷiṟ cila kīḻē tarappaṭukiṉṟaṉa;
 (1) kirēkka moḻiyiṟpōl ṉakaram uṭampaṭu meyyākap payaṉpaṭutal.
(2) mūṉṟām iṭattilum (paṭarkkaiyilum), viṉaic collilum pāl pākupāṭu iruttal; ciṟappākap potuppāl (oṉṟaṉpāl) eṉa oṉṟiruttal.
(3) paṭarkkai yorumai oṉṟaṉpāṟ peyariṉ vikuti `t’ āka iruttal.
(4) ilattīṉaip pōṉṟu palaviṉpāl vikutī akaramā yiruttal.
(5) tolaic cuṭṭu akaramākavum, aṇmaiccuṭṭu ikaramākavum iruttal.
(6) pārasīka moḻi pōṉṟu iṟantakāla maṟivikkap perumpālum `t’ eṉṟa aṭaiyai (iṭainilaiyai)p payaṉpaṭuttal.
(7) pakutiyiṉ oru kūṟṟai iraṭṭittu iṟantakālam uṇartal.
(8) viṉaippakutiyiṉ mutaluyir nīṇṭu toḻiṟ peyarātal.
mēṟkāṭṭiya cila pakutikaḷālēyē intu-airōppiya- tirāviṭat toṭarpu oṉṟu uḷḷatu eṉpatu pulappaṭum. aṉṟiyum, tirāviṭa moḻikaḷukku arukiṟ paṉṉeṭunāḷāyp payiṉṟuvanta moḻi vaṭamoḻiyē yāyiṉum, tirāviṭa mutaṟ coṟkaḷiṟ pala vaṭamoḻi allatu kīḻa intu - airōppiyak kuḻuvaiviṭa mēla airōppiyak kuḻuvaiyē cārntavaiyā yuḷḷaṉa eṉṟa kūṟṟiṉ uṇmaiyum viḷaṅkum. eṉavē, tirāviṭa moḻiyaic cittiya iṉac cārpuṭaiyateṉa vakuttuk koḷvatē cariyāyiṉum, antac cittiya iṉattiluḷḷa maṟṟellā moḻikaḷaiyum viṭa intu-airōppiya moḻi kaḷuṭaṉ mikap palavāṉa, paḻaimaiyāṉa ariya oppumaikaḷai yuṭaiya kuḻu tirāviṭak kuḻuvē eṉpatu teḷivākum. ik kuḻuvai āriya cittiya iṉaṅkaḷukku iṭaippaṭṭa kuḻu eṉṟu collāmaṟ pōṉāluṅkūṭa, intu-airōppiya iṉattuṭaṉ uṟavuṭaiya cittiya uṟuppu moḻi eṉṟēṉum kūṟat taṭaiyillai. inta iraṇṭu iṉaṅkaḷiṉ mikap paḻaimaiyāṉa orumaippāṭṭukkum ituvē cāṉṟākum. ik koḷkai cariyāyiṉ, tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiluḷḷa intu-airōppiya oṟṟumaikaḷ nammai varalāṟṟuk kālaṅkaḷ aṉaittiṟkum muṟpaṭṭa kālattiṟkuk koṇṭu cellukiṉṟaṉa; atumaṭṭumō? camayak kataikaḷiṉ tōṟṟa kālattiṟkum muntiyum, intu-airōppiya iṉattār mēlaiiṉam, kīḻaiiṉam eṉap pirivataṟkum muntiyum, intu - airōppiya iṉattāṉum, iṉṟu cittiyac cārpuṭaiyavai eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum ellā moḻi kaḷukkum tāyakamāṉa mutaṟ cittiya moḻi yiṉattuṭaṉ oṉṟupaṭṭirunta kālattiṟku muntiyum koṇṭucellun takaiyavā yirukkiṉṟaṉa.
tirāviṭa moḻikaḷil, ciṟappākat tamiḻil, orucila cemittiya oṟṟumaikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟamai viyakkattakkatē. avaṟṟuḷḷum cila oṟṟumaikaḷ ivviraṇṭil maṭṭumaṉṟi, epirēyattilum, intu-airōppiya moḻikaḷilum irukkiṉṟaṉa. ittakaiya iṭaṅkaḷil epirēya uruvē intu-airōppiya uruviṉum tirāviṭa moḻikaḷukku aṇmaiyatā yirukkiṉṟatu. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka, tamiḻil avā (peyarccol); āval (toḻiṟ peyar); ivaṟṟai ilattīṉam ave-ō (viṉaippakuti), vaṭamoḻi av mutaliyavai aṇṭiyē irukkiṉṟaṉa. āyiṉum, epirēyam avvāh (peyarccol), āvah (viṉai) eṉṟa iraṇṭum mikavum aṇmaiyuṭaiyavā yirukkiṉṟamai kāṇka! iṅṅaṉam tirāviṭam uḷḷaṭaṅkiya ellā iṉaṅkaḷukkum potuvāṉa oṟṟumaikaḷ orupuṟam irukka, tirāviṭam, epirēyam ivaṟṟiṟkē ciṟappāy, piṟa moḻikaḷ evaṟṟiṟkum uriyavallāta oppumaip paṇpukaḷum cila irukkiṉṟaṉa.
mēṟ kuṟippiṭṭa tamiḻiluḷḷa cemittiya oṟṟumaikaḷ intu - airōppiya oṟṟumaikaḷaip pōlac ciṟantavaiyumalla, palavu malla; āṉāl, im mūṉṟiṉaṅkaḷiluṅ kāṇappeṟum oṟṟumaikaḷuṭaṉum, epirēyattuṭaṉ mikavum neruṅkiyavāyk kāṇappeṟum paṇpukaḷuṭaṉum cērntu, avai tirāviṭa moḻiyiṉ paṇṭait toṉmaiyaip paṟṟi ārāyum aṟiñarukku mikavum payaṉ tarat takkavai. ittakaiya oppumai kaḷai iṅkē kuṟippatu tirāviṭattiṟkum epirēyattiṟkum ivaṟṟiṟkum toṭarpu uṇṭeṉṟu kāṭṭavaṉṟu; “pala maram kaṇṭa taccaṉ oru maramum veṭṭamāṭṭāṉ” eṉṟa uṇmaiyaik karuttiṟ koṇṭu ittuṟaikaḷil ellām ārāycciyaic celuttāviṭiṉum, piṟaratu ārāyccikkāka ittakaiya muṭivu paṭuttappaṭāta citaṟiya oppumaikaḷai appaṭiyē eṭuttuk kāṭṭavēyām. āṉāl, intu- airōppiya tirāviṭa oppumaikaḷ moḻiyiyaliṉ uyirnilaiyai oṭṭiyavaiyātaliṉ avaṟṟaip paḻaimaiyāṉa kūṭṭuṟaviṉāl peṟappaṭṭavai eṉṟu kūṟiviṭa muṭiyātu. cemittiya oṟṟumaikaḷō iṅṅaṉamaṉṟi iṉa oṟṟumaiyai iṉṟiyamaiyātavaiyāka vēṇṭakkūṭiyavai yalla; taṟceyalāy ēṟpaṭṭavai eṉṟō, allatu ētēṉum orukālattil paṇṭait tirāviṭarkaḷ cemittiya iṉattāruṭaṉ aṭuttu vāḻntamaiyāl ēṟpaṭṭavai (itu eḷitil iruntirukkak kūṭiyatē) eṉṟō kūṟiviṭalām.
iṉi, tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiṉ iṭappeyarkaḷ teṟku, mēṟku āstirēliyāviluḷḷa paḻañkuṭikaḷiṉ moḻiyiṟ kāṇappeṟum iṭappeyarkaḷai viyakkattakka muṟaiyil ottirukkiṉṟaṉa. iv voppumaikkuk kāraṇaṅkaḷ pala kūṟalāmāyiṉum, oppumai irukkiṉṟa teṉpatai maṭṭilum maṟuttaṟkillai. āṉāl, āstirēliyat taṉmai iṭappeyar, tirāviṭamoḻit taṉmai iṭappeyaraiviṭa, tipēttiya moḻit taṉmaip peyarai mikutiyum ottuḷḷatu eṉpatu itukāṟuṅ kavaṉikkappaṭavillai. kīḻvarum oppumaip paṭṭiyai nōkkuka!
	tirāviṭam 	āsti	tipēttiyam	cīṉam			rēliyam
taṉmai	nāṉ,yāṉ	ṅa,ṅaii,	ṅa,ṅe,ṅet,	ṅo
orumai	nā,eṉ	ṅatsa,ṅaṉya	
itil āstirēliyat taṉmaip peyariṉ pakuti tipēttiya moḻiyaiyum intu-cīṉa iṉattaiyum peritum ottiruppiṉum, paṉmaiyākum poḻuta atu tirāviṭa moḻikaḷai, atilum ciṟappākat teluṅku moḻiyai, ottirukkiṟatu. teluṅkil paṉmai lu eṉṟa vikuti cērppatāl ēṟpaṭukiṟatu. atēpōṉṟu āstirēliya moḻikaḷilum lu, li, ṭalu, ṭli ivaṟṟaic cērppatāl paṉmai ēṟpaṭukiṟatu. intiyāviṉ vaṭakiḻakkiluḷḷa moḻikaḷum ivvakaiyil teluṅkai ottuḷḷaṉa. eṭuttuk kāṭṭāka, timal29 nā (= nī) eṉpataṉ paṉmai niyel (=nīr, nīṅkaḷ) eṉpatām. taṉmaiyilum āstirēliya moḻiyiṉ irumaip peyarkaḷ, paṉmaip peyarkaḷ ṅalu, ṅaṭlu, ṅaṭli, ṅalata eṉpaṉa pōṉṟavai . itaṉuṭaṉ teluṅkup peyarkaḷ paṉmaiyākum muṟaiyaik kūrntu nōkkuka. (e-ṭu) : vā(ṇ)ṭu (=avaṉ), vā(ṇ)ṭlu (=avarkaḷ); tamiḻiluṅkūṭa `nāṉ’ eṉpataṉ paṉmaiyāṉa `nāṅkaḷ’ itaṉai mikavum aṭuttiruppatu kāṇka!
āstirēliya muṉṉilai iṭappeyarkaḷum, tirāviṭa muṉṉilai iṭappeyarkaḷum taṉmai, paṉmai iraṇṭilum teḷivāka ottirukkiṉṟaṉa. atilum vikutiyuṭaṉmaṭṭumaṉṟip pakutiyilum muḻu oṟṟumai iruppatuṅ kāṇka! tirāviṭa moḻikaḷil potuppaṭaiyāṉa (muṉṉilaip) peyarkaḷiṉ orumai nīṉ eṉpatum, paṉmai nīm eṉpatum ām. ivaṟṟuḷ nī eṉpatē pakutip poruḷ taruvatu. i/tu īreṇkaḷilum oṉṟākavē irukkiṟatu. orumaiyil orumai vikutiyāka ṉakaramum (ṉ), paṉmaiyil paṉmai vikutiyāka makaramum (m) varukiṉṟaṉa. cila iṭaṅkaḷil paṉmai vikutiyākiya marakattiṟku māṟākap paṭarkkaip paṉmaiyiṉ vikutiyākiya rakaram vantu nīr (teluṅku mīru; atāvatu mīr+u) ātalum uṇṭu. āṉāl, putitāka vanta inta rakara vikuti eḻuvāyil, atilum taṉiyāṉacollil maṭṭumē varukiṟatu. piṟa vikutikaḷ urupukaḷ cērnta pakupataṅkaḷuḷ varumpoḻutu makara vikutiyē varutal kāṇka! (e-ṭu): nāṅkaḷ = nām +kaḷ; nīṅkaḷ = nīm+kaḷ; uṅkaḷ ummuṭaiya mutalāyiṉa. orumaiyil muṉṉilaip peyariluḷḷa uyiroli ikaramēyāyiṉum, paṉmaiyil ikarattiṉiṭamāka ukaramum varukiṟatu. (e-ṭu): pirakuvi; num = nīṅkaḷ; centamiḻ: num (uṅkaḷuṭaiya). ik kālak kaṉṉaṭam in “nīm” eṉpataṉ makarattai vakaramāka melivittu, nīu30 allatu nīvu31 ākkukiṉṟatu kāṇka! immāṟṟaṅkaḷ ovvoṉṟilum ivvāṟu āstirēliya moḻiyiyalpu tirāviṭa moḻi yiyalpai muṟṟilum ottiruppatu viḷakka muṭiyātatoru putumaiyēyām.
	tirāviṭam 	āstirēliyam
muṉṉilai		niṉṉa,ṅiṉṉe,
orumai	nīṉ, niṉ	ṅintoa, niṅte.
muṉṉilaip	nīm,nim	nimetū, nura,	paṉmai	nīr, num	niva, ṅurle.			nīvu
kīḻvarum oppumaippaṭṭiyil irumai, paṉmai ākiya īreṇkaḷum (iraṇṭiṉ poruḷum tirāviṭamoḻikaḷil oṉṟēyātalāl) paṉmai eṉṟē kuṟikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
tamiḻ taṉmai orumai iraṇṭām vēṟṟumai urupu `eṉṉai’ eṉpataiyum āstirēliya iraṇṭām vēṟṟumai uru `eṉmo’ eṉpataiyum oppunōkkuka!
āstirēliya moḻikaḷiṉ moḻiyamaippu cittiya iṉattuṭaṉ perumpālum oṟṟumaiyuṭaiyatākavē kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.muṉṉaṭaikaḷiṉ iṭamākap piṉṉaṭaikaḷai vaḻaṅkutal; taṉmaip paṉmaiyil oṉṟu muṉṉilai nīṅkalākavum oṉṟu ataṉai uḷappaṭuttiyum iraṇṭu vaṭivaṅkaḷ uṭaimai; taṉviṉai, piṟaviṉai mutaliyavaṟṟai vikuti cērppatālēyē terivittal; coṟkaḷiṉ aṭukkiyal amaippu, vākkiyat tiluḷḷa coṟṟoṭarpu muṟai ākiya ivai yaṉaittilum ivai tirāviṭa moḻikaḷaiyum, turukkiyam maṅkōliyam pōṉṟa piṟa cittiya moḻikaḷaiyum ottē irukkiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl, oṉṟiraṇṭu iṭaṅkaḷ nīṅkalāka, `pāliṉēsiya’32 moḻikaḷuṭaṉ ivai vēṟupaṭukiṉṟaṉa. āstirēliya moḻikaḷil tiraṭṭappaṭṭa coṟṟokutikaḷil ivvoṟṟumaikaḷai uṟutippaṭuttum vēṟu vivaraṅkaḷ illaiyāyiṉum, ivvoṟṟumaikaḷ taṟceyalāṉavai yeṉṟu viṭattakkavaiyalla.
naṭu āppirikkāvil pōrṉu33 nāṭṭil pēcappaṭum kaṉūri34 allatu pōrṉu eṉṉum moḻikkum tirāviṭa moḻikkum toṭarpiruppatu mēṟkūṟiya oppumaiyiṉum putumaiyāṉatēyām. iv voppumaikaḷ potuppaṭai yāṉavai eṉpatu uṇmaiyē. avaiyāvaṉa: kaṉūriyum “aṭukkiyal” amaip puṭaiyatu uṟavu aṭaikaḷai muṉṉaṭaiyākkāmal piṉṉaṭai yākkukiṟatu; aiyam, viṉā, aḻuttam mutaliyavaṟṟaik kuṟikka (tirāviṭamoḻi kaḷukkē ciṟappāṉa muṟaiyil) aṭaikaḷaip peyar viṉaikaḷuṭaṉ cērkkiṟatu; viṉaikku etirmaṟai uruvum uṇṭu haṅkēriya moḻiyaippōṉṟu taṉviṉai35 piṟaviṉai36 uṇṭu. tirāviṭa moḻikaḷuṭaṉ mika neruṅkiya oṟṟumai iruppataik kuṟippatu muṉṉilai orumaiyākiya `ni’ eṉpatākum. ituvum tirāviṭam pirākuvi, cīṉam, pehistaṉ aṭṭavaṇai moḻi, āstirēliyam mutaliya moḻikaḷ aṉaittiṟkum potuvāṉateṉpatu mēlē kāṭṭappaṭṭatu. kaṉūri moḻi mēla āppirikkāviṉ moḻikaḷiliruntu mikutiyum vēṟṟumaip paṭuvatāl atu taṉippaṭṭa ārāyccikkuriyatākum.37
