teṉṉintiya moḻikaḷiṉ tiṟamuṇarntu kālṭuvel aiyar naṟṟamiḻ moḻiyai nāṭṭu makkaḷ pēcum muṟaiyilēyē pēcak karutiṉār. eḻuttu naṭaikkum pēccu naṭaikkum uḷḷa vēṟṟumaiyai naṉkuṇarntu pēccu muṟaikkuriya tamiḻaip peritum muyaṉṟu paḻakiṉār. appaḻakkattiṉ payaṉāka avar pēciya tamiḻ, kēṭṭārp piṇikkum takaiyatāy amain tiruntatu.  nūl naṭaiyil vaḻaṅkum tiri coṟkaḷum kallār nāvil naṭamāṭum koccai moḻikaḷum, aiyar pēcciṟ kāṇappaṭāmaiyāl avar virivurai kaṟṟārkkum kallārkkum oruṅkē iṉimaiyum payaṉum viḷaippatāyiṟṟu. ēcu matattiṉarallātārum avar pēccaik kēṭka ēkkamuṟṟirun tārkaḷ. poruṉai yāṟṟiṉ karaiyilamainta āḻvār tirunakari eṉṉum ciṟṟūrkku aiyar orumuṟai ceṉṟapōtu, avvūr vaitika mutiyōr avarait tam ālaya muṉ maṇṭapattiṟku aḻaittuc ceṉṟu virivurai nikaḻtta vēṇṭiṉāreṉṟāl avar colliṉ cemmaikku vēṟu cāṉṟum vēṇṭamō!
ittakaiya arumpulamai vāynta aiyar naṇparuṭaṉ uraiyāṭum poḻutu nakaiccuvai tatumpap pēcuvār. oru nāḷ kalkattā nakariṉiṉṟu viruntāka vantaṭainta ‘kāṭṭaṉ’ eṉṉum camaya kuruvai aḻaittuk koṇṭu aiyar kaṉṉiyā kumarikkuc ceṉṟār. nīlattiraik kaṭalōrattilē niṉṟu iruvarum neṭuṅ kaṭaliṉ nīrmaiyai neṭitu nōkki viyappuṟṟār. teṉ tamiḻ nāṭṭil ellaiyil niṉṟu kāval puriyum kaṉṉiyiṉ tavap perumaiyaṟintu poṉ maṇalum, karumaṇalum, veṇ maṇaloṭu kalantu tūvi aṭi paṇinta karuṅkaṭaliṉ perumai kaṇṭu kaḷippuṟṟār. appōtu vaṅka nāṭṭup periyār kumarit tuṟaiyiliṟaṅki nīrāṭa virumpiṉār. avar viruppattiṟ kiṇaṅkiya kālṭuvel aiyar attuṟaiyil kuḷittaṟkuriya muṟaikaḷaik kuṟippāka eṭutturaittuk kāṭṭaṉ kaiyaip paṟṟik kumarik kōliṉ etirēyamainta tuṟaiyiliṟaṅkiṉār. kaṭalil nīrāṭip paḻakāta kāṭṭaṉ, nurait teḻunta tiraikkaṭaliṉ āravāraṅ kaṇṭu añci aiyar karattai iṟukap paṟṟiṉār. appoḻutu perumita muṟṟeḻunta vanta alai yoṉṟu kāṭṭaṉaip pirittiḻuttup puraṭṭik karaiyil uruṭṭiyatu. alaiyiṉ koṭumaik kāṟṟātu amainta kāṭṭaṉai aiyar viraintōṭik kavarntu karuṅkaṭaliṉ vāyiṉiṉṟum viṭuvittār. karuṇaiyaṟṟa alaiyiṉ kaiyakap paṭṭu nilaikulainta kāṭṭaṉai nōkki, ‘aiyaṉē! paṇpuṟṟa num perumai aṟintu pakavati calām ceytuviṭṭāḷ; oru calām pōtum; ippoḻutē karaiyēṟiviṭuvōm’ eṉṟu kūṟi aṭutta alai varumuṉṉē avarai aiyar appuṟap paṭuttiṉār. iṅṅaṉam kumarittuṟaiyil calām peṟṟa kāṭṭaṉ, kalkattā nakaril kaṅkaiyāṟṟil tavaṟi vīḻntu uyir tuṟantāreṉṟu aṟintapōtu kālṭuvel aiyar peruntuyara muṟṟār.
ceyaṟkarum paṇi ceyta ciṟappuṟṟa kālṭuvel aiyar maṉamoḻi meyyeṉṉum mūṉṟaiyum tamiḻakattārkkē oppuvitta taṉmaiyāl apperiyārait tamiḻar eṉṟu karututalē takkatākum. avarum avvaṇṇamē karutiṉar eṉpatu avar vāḻkkaik kātaiyāl viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. tam piṟappaiyum vaḷarppaiyum kuṟittu eḻutiya kātaiyil “nāṉ ayarlāntu tēcattiṟ piṟantēṉ; skāṭlaṇṭu tēcattil vaḷarntēṉ; āṅkila nūlkaḷil āḻntēṉ; āyiṉum eṉ vāḻnāḷil aimpatāṇṭukaṭku mēlākap pāratap perunāṭum annāṭṭu makkaḷumē eṉ karuttai muṟṟuṅ kavarntu koṇṭamaiyāl yāṉ intiya makkaḷuḷ oruvaṉāyiṉēṉ.”2 eṉṟu tam maṉattileḻunta uṇarcciyai māṇpuṟa viḷakkip pōntār.
karuṇai uruvāya kālṭuvellaiyar poruṉai nāṭṭait tammiruppiṭamākat tērnta kālamutal tamiḻ makkaḷuṭaṉ vēṟṟumaiyiṉṟik kalantuṟaivārāyiṉar. aṟiyāmaiyil mūḻkik kiṭanta māntarkkuk kalaiyiṉ oḷi kāṭṭiṉār. kirustu camayattai avariṭaiyē nalamuṟa nāṭṭiṉār. allatu pakalum iṭaiyaṟā tuḻaittuvanta nellai nāṭṭilēyē kallaṟai koḷḷa virumpiṉār.3 tēri nilattiṉ vemmaiyār aiyar uṭal nalaṅkuṉṟak kaṇṭa āṅkila nāṭṭu naṇparkaḷ avaraik kuḷinta nāṭṭiṟku varumāṟu paṉmuṟai ātarittu aḻaittārkaḷ. aṉṉār viruppattiṟ kiṇaṅki aiyar āṅkila nāṭṭiṟku mummuṟai ceṉṟārēṉum, avar maṉam poruṉai nāṭṭaiyē nāṭuvatāyiṟṟu. nalivuṟṟavuṭal nalamuṟṟavuṭaṉ cēyai niṉaittu cellum tāypōl tērikkuṭikaḷai nōkki viraintu pōntār.
mutaṉ muṟai4 ceṉṟapōtu āṅkila nāṭṭil mūvāṇṭu taṅkiṉār. iraṇṭām muṟai5 ceṉṟapōtu īrāṇṭu taṅkiṉār. mūṉṟām muṟai6 ceṉṟapōtu ōrāṇṭu taṅki mīṇṭār. itaṉai ārāyum poḻutu, uṭal nalampēṇac ceṉṟa viṭattum avar maṉam tamiḻakattaiyē nāṭiya teṉpatum, mēṉiyiṉ taḷarviṉāl mutumai viraintuṟutalai aṟinta aiyar āṅkila nāṭṭil avappoḻutu pōkkak karutātu tamiḻakam pōntu viḻumiya tavappaṇi āṟṟa viḻaintāreṉpatum viḷaṅkit tōṉṟum. āyiṉum mūṉṟām muṟai cīmaikkuc ceṉṟu tirumpiyapoḻutu avaruṭal kaṭṭiḻantu ulaintu taḷarvuṟṟatu. eḻupatāṇṭu kaṇṭa kiḻavar poruṉai nāṭṭiṉ vemmaiyaip poṟukkalāṟṟātu vetumpiṉār. annilaiyil koṭaikkāṉal malaikkuc ceṉṟu ataṉ kuḷirmaiyaiyum ceḻumaiyaiyum nukarntu maṉantaḻaittār. ammalaiyiṟ cila kālam taṅkiyirukka eṇṇiṉār. iyaṟkai vaḷam malinta kōṭai malaiyil iṟaivaṉai vaḻupaṭutaṟkuriya tirukkōyil illāmai kaṇṭu maṉam varuntiṉār. mutumaip paruvattil orumaiyuṭaṉiruntu iṟaivaṉ tiruvaruḷai niṉaintu vaḻipaṭutaṟkuriya ālayameṭukka viḻaintār. aracāṅkattil ciṟanta celvā kkuṭaiyarāyirunta ‘laviñcu’ turaiyiṭam aiyar taṅkaruttai aṟivittapōtu avar ūkkamuṟṟut tirukkōvil amaittaṟkuriya iṭattait tērntutaviyatōṭu ciṟanta naṉkoṭaiyum vaḻaṅkiṉār. aṉpārnta viyattar ātaravait tuṇaik koṇṭu ammalaiyil aiyar aḻakiya kōvil kaṭṭi muṭittu iṟaivaṉukku arppaṇañ ceytār. avviḻāvil aiyar iyaṟṟiya virivurai ciṟanta aṟivuraiyākat tikaḻkiṉṟatu. avar eṭuttu muṭitta tirukkōvil cīruñ ciṟappumuṟṟuk koṭaikkāṉaṟ kuṉṟil iṉṟum viḷaṅkak kāṇalām.7
aṭikkuṟippu
1.	“haḷa hna aṭienaṉa yabṭirava kacaṭia ṉaḷaivacaiuva vaṭi ṉaḷaivacaiuva hna ṟayaḷa anava ṭin vāna ṟayala yaṉē ṟanaṭauṭianaṉa yava nayauh hyaṭavaipē yīṭayauna le ucaṭiṟaṉaḷa ṭika yīnaṭiyīṭana ṟaivā vāna  raḷarayaṭa yauuṭiayīyanianavē ṭika yeṉē, yennēcaḷa yaṉē vaṭicauhṭaipāva yīcaṭiunaḷaḷaiṭiḷē. hakavanaca ya ṭaṭipē yaṉē vacainaḷaṭiana taṭiracanēla,  vāna vayaayaḷāya nepayan ṟaivā yaṉaṉacanaḷaḷanaḷa yaṉē ḷaṭipēḷa yaṉē vāna kṣaiḷāṭiyī canayīṭanaiṉa ṟaivā rakēṭayapapaipē yīyavanainuna. ḻana ḷayava ṭaḷaivananipē le vāna hṭiraca vaṭi yaṭaṭa vāyava vānala hyaṉa vaṭi ḷayala yaṉē yayīyīnayacanaṉa yaḷa hyayīyīla yaṉē yīhṭaiṭiḷaṭiyīhiu yaḷa kai vāna ṉaraḷava yaṉē hnayava, vāna ṉain ṭika vaṭiavaṭiaḷa, vāna ṭeyacana ṭika vāna ṉacanayaṉakaraṭa ceyaṇanan iḷēvacaraanavēḷa yaṉē vāna ḷaanaṭaṭa ṭika vāna ṭiṭai vaṭicauhnaḷa ṟanacana yaḷa canakacanaḷāipē yaḷa vāna yevā yaṉē ṉainnēca vāyava ṟanacana yaṟayavaiipē hia. nuenan in hḷai ṭiṭaṉa yapana ṟānan hna uṭiraṭaṉa ḷauyacauvaila ḷanana vaṭi canayaṉa, hna ḷavaṭiṭiṉa nacanauva yaḷa ya yīyaṭaa, vāna nayīḷaiuṭiyīyaṭa enaḷavaanavē, vāna ṭaṭipē ṟāvaina hyacai, vāna ḷanṭiṟala neyacaṉa yaṉē vāna uṭanayaca urava kanayavaracanaḷa paieipē yan ṉainayaṭa yīcanaḷanavaanavē ṭika ya enacanēcayaṭena biḷāṭiyī.” - ū.ū. 241. pu
2.	“āla canaḷaṉainanuna in aiṉēyai kaṭica le kayaca vāna ṭayacapanaca yīṭicavaiṭin ṭika ala  ṭakaina yaṉē vāna ṉananayī ivēnacanaḷava ai hyaena yaṭaṟayalaḷa vayamanan in aiṉēyai yaṉē naenacalavāpai aiṉēyain āyamanaḷa ana aṭicana yan aiṉēyain vāyan yalēvāipē naṭaḷana.” - ū.cu. 3
3.	“aiva hyaṉa ṭaṭipē nenan vāna naoyīcanaḷaḷanaṉa ṟaivā ṭika vāna biḷāṭiyī vāyava ṟānan hna ṉanaiṉa hna ḷaṭiraṭaṉa ne recanaiṉa yaaṭipē hḷai yīnaṭiyīṭana. ain kayauva ḷaṭi ṭaṭipē yapaṭi yaḷa 1883 ṟānan hna ṟayaḷa ṭayaḷava in nupēṭayaṉē yaṉē ṟayaḷa canaḷūranaḷavanaṉa le hḷai kacanaiṉēḷa vaṭi canaayain vānacana, hḷai yaḷēṟanaca ṟayaḷa “ai ṟaiḷā vaṭi ṉanai yaaṭipēnaḷava vāna yīnaṭiyīṭana kaṭica ṟāṭia ai hyaena ṭaienaṉa.” - cunae. ṟulayavava ū.cu. 186.
4.	1854 - 1857.
5.	1873 - 1875
6.	1883 - 1884
7.	ḷuva. ñanavanaca’ḷa ūhracauh - ṉunaṉaiuyavanaṉa 1886.
14. mūppum muṭivum
aimpattu mūṉṟāṇṭa tamiḻ nāṭṭil arumpaṇi purinta kālṭuvel aiyar paḻutta mutumaiyuṟṟār. nalamelām oruṅkē amainta mukattil naraiyum tiraiyum niṟaintaṉa. parantuyarnta neṟṟiṉ mītu pañcu pōl viḷaṅkiya talaiyum, paṇpuṟṟa neñciṉ nilaikuṟitta pālpōṉṟa tāṭiyum mūttavaṭi vaḻakiṉ perumaiyai eḻutik kāṭṭiṉa. kaṟṟōrkkaṇiyāya naṟṟavap periyār ceṟṟam nīkkiya maṉattiṉarāy mācaṟa imaikkum uruviṉarāyt tikaḻntār.
“valamsapari puraiyum vāṉarai muṭiyiṉar
  mācaṟa imaikkum uruviṉar...
  ceṟṟam nīkkiya maṉattiṉar...
  kaṟṟōr yāvarum aṟiyā aṟiviṉar”
eṉṟu nakkīrar aruḷiya muṉivar kōlam naṟṟavac celvarāya kālṭuvel aiyariṭam muṟṟum amaintu viḷaṅkiṟṟu eṉṟu kūṟutal mikaiyākātu. vemmai cāṉṟa nilattil viḻuppaṇi purintu paḻutta aiyar, ceṉṉai attiyaṭca kuruviṉiṭam muṟaiyiṭṭup poṟuppu vāynta vēlaiyiṉiṉṟum vilaki iḷaippāṟa virumpiṉār.
“aiya cālavum alaciṉaṉ arumperu mūppum veyyatāyatu” ātalāl vilakiṭa utaviṭa vēṇṭum eṉṟu apperiyāraip parintu vēṇṭiṉār. kālṭuvel aṟiñariṉ arumaiyai naṉkaṟinta attiyaṭca kuru avar viruppattiṟ kiṇaṅki iḷaippāṟa viṭai koṭuttār. poṟuppum perumaiyum vāynta arumpaṇi tuṟantu puṉitarāy aiyar koṭaikkāṉaṟ kuṉṟil vāḻap puṟappaṭṭapōtu iṭaiyaṉkuṭi vaṭṭamainta makkaḷaṉaivarum aṇiyaṇiyākap pōntu pirivāṟṟātu kalaṅki aḻutu kataṟat talaippaṭṭār. aḻuta kaṇṇiṉarāy aṭipaṇinta māntarai aiyar akaṉamarntu taḻuvi eṭuttu āci kūṟiṉār. toḻuta kaiyiṉarāyt tuyaruṟṟu niṉṟa mātarait tūyanaṉmoḻikaḷāl tēṟṟiyaruḷiṉār. oruvāṟu piriveṉum peruntuyar poṟutta makkaḷpāl viṭaipeṟṟuk koṭaikkāṉal malaiyai nōkkic ceṉṟār.
annāḷil koṭaikkāṉaṟ kuṉṟiṟku nēriyacālai amaikkappaṭavillai. kaṭumpāṟaiyiṉ vaḻiyākak kātavaḻi tūram neṭuntaṭaṅ kiṭantatu. ceṅkuttāka nivanta pāṟai kaḷiṉiṭaiyē neḷintu ceṉṟa neṭuntaṭam vaḻi naṭantu celvārkku varuttam viḷaittatu. ittakaiya taṭattiṉ vaḻiyākat tam maṉaiviyoṭu naṭantu koṭaikkāṉaṟ kuṉṟaṭainta aiyar kavalai nīttu iḷaippāṟik kaḷippuṟṟār. koṭaikkāṉaliṉ kuḷirmaiyāl aiyar mēṉitaḷarvu nīṅkit taḻaittatu. ennāḷum ēcuviṉ naṉṉāmam maṟantaṟiyāta aiyar akkuṉṟilamainta tiruk kōvilil vārantoṟum tavaṟātu ceṉṟu vaḻipāṭupaṟṟiyum, virivurai nikaḻttiyum amaitiyuṟṟu vāḻntār. aṟamē uruvāya aiyar maṉaiviyār poḻutaṟintu ēṟṟa uṇavarutti avar mēṉiyaip poṉpōṟ pōṟṟi vantār. nāṭōṟum kālaiyum mālaiyum aiyar neṭuntūram naṭantu malaivaḷaṅ kaṇṭu makiḻntār.
iṅṅaṉam vāḻuṅkāl orunāḷ veḷiyē ceṉṟu ulāvi vanta aiyar kuḷiruṟṟu naṭuṅkiṉār. ammalaiyil vāḻnta maruttuvar oruvar viraintu pōntu maruntaḷittār. mutaliṟ ciṟitu maṭaṅkat toṭaṅkiya nōy ēḻām nāḷ valuvuṟṟu varuttat toṭaṅkiyatu. iravu muḻutum, kālṭuvel aiyar piṇiyiṉ koṭumaiyāl vāṭi varuntiṉār. āstirēliyā viṉiṉṟu tantaiyaik kāṇa vantirunta āṭiṅkitaṉeṉṉum arumaicāṉṟa maintaṉ kaṅkul kaḻiyumaḷavum kaṇṇayarātu aiyar paṭukkaiyiṉarukē amarntu paṇiviṭai purintār. tam vāḻkkaiyiṉ ellai vantuṟṟateṉṟu aṟinta aiyar karuṇai yaṅ kaṭalāya karttaraik karuttilamaittu, maṟaimoḻikaḷāl maṉamārattoḻutu, “puṇṇiyā! uṉṉiṭamē pōtukiṉ ṟēṉ” eṉṟuraittu maṇṇulaka vāḻvai muṭittār.
tirucciyilirunta marumakarukku aiyar iṟantār eṉṉum ceyti tantiyiṉ vaḻiyāka aṟivikkappaṭṭatu. avar uṭaṉē puṟappaṭṭu iṭaiyaṉkuṭikkuc ceṉṟu aiyar eṭutta ālayattiṟ kallaṟaiyamaittaṟkuriya vēlaiyil īṭupaṭṭār. āyiṉum, koṭaikkāṉal malaiyil nikaḻnta koṭumaiyai annilaiyil ūrārkku aṟivittal ollāteṉṟu karuti uṟṟa paṇiyāḷar cilariṭam maṟaivākak kūṟi aṭakkivaittār.
aiyar iṟanta ceyti koṭaikkāṉaṟ kuṉṟil paraviya poḻutu āṇṭirunta aṟiñaraṉaivarum avar tirumēṉiyaik kāṇa viraintu moyttār. aiyar mukattilamainta cāntamum teḷivuṅ kaṇṭu vaṇaṅkiṉār. periyavar uṭalaip pōṟṟi eṭuttup pēḻaiyuḷaṭakkik kaṇṇīrum kaḷimalarum cintiṉār. pēḻaiyiṉmītu aṉpariṭṭa mālaikaḷ aṭukkaṭukkāyk kuvintaṉa, kātavaḻi tūram kaṭum pātaiyiṉ vaḻiyākap pēḻaiyai eṭuttup perum paṇiyāḷar vēṇṭumē eṉṟu aiyar maṉaiviyār kavalaiyuṟṟār. appoḻutu kaṇkaḷil nīrum kaikaḷil malarum tāṅki ōraṉ parkuḻām pēḻaiyai nōkki vantatu. toṇṭa rāya aiyarai aḻaittuc cellat tēvatūtar vantaṉarō eṉṟu ammaiyār aiyuṟṟār. karttarai nampiṉōr eññāṉṟum kaiviṭappaṭār eṉṉum mutumoḻiyai niṉaittu avaraiyē nōkki niṉṟār.
akaṅkuḻaintu vantaṭainta aṉparkaḷ aiyar pēḻaiyai aṇukik kaṇṇīrai malaroṭu kalantu corintārkaḷ. appāl aṟamē uruvāyamainta ammaiyiṉ aṭi paṇinteḻuntu, “tāyē! aruntava muṉivarāya aiyaraic cumantu cellum perumaiyai ēḻaikaḷāya eṅkaḷukku aḷittal vēṇṭum” eṉṟu iṟaiñci niṉṟār. aṟṟārkku uṟṟa tuṇaiyāy niṉṟu aruḷ puriyum iṟaivaṉ karuṇaiyai amimāyār niṉaittu maṉamāra vaḻuttip pēḻaiyai mella eṭuttuc cellumāṟu aṉparaip paṇintu vēṇṭiyāṉaḷ. appaṇi talaimēṟ koṇṭa aṟunāṉ kaṭiyavar aruṅ kuḻaviyait taḻuviyeṭukkum tāypōl pēḻaiyai ātarit teṭuttut talaimicait tāṅki naṭantārkaḷ. appēḻaiyiṉ piṉṉē aiyar maṉaiviyum maintaṉum cintaṉai mukattiṟ tēkki vantaṉai purintu naṭantu ceṉṟār. kātavaḻi tūramum pātaiyiṉ kaṭumaiyaṟiyātu aiyar pēḻaiyai aluṅkāmaṟ kuluṅkāmaṟ koṇṭu ceṉṟa aṭiyāratu aṉpiṉ tiṟaṅkaṇṭu piṉṉē naṭanta iruvarum peruviyap puṟṟār. malaiyaṭi vāraṅ kaṭanta piṉṉar aruñcumaiyai nāṭṭu vaṇṭiyilēṟṟi iruppup pātai nilaiyattiṟ koṇṭu cērttārkaḷ. naṇ pakaṟ poḻutil annilaiyattu vantuṟṟa rayil vaṇṭiyil pēḻaiyai ēṟṟi aiyar maṉaiviyum maintaṉum arukēyiruntu kāval purintaṉar.
aiyaruṭalait tāṅki viraintu naṭanta vaṇṭi anti mālaiyil nellai mānakarai aṭaintatu. nellai nilaiyattil kāruṭait toṇṭar palar kāttiruntaṉar. aiyar pēḻaiyai vaṇṭiyiṉiṉṟeṭuttapōtu aṉpar aṉaivarum kaṇmaḻai poḻintār. teṇṇīr teḷittaṟkuriya taraiyiṟ kaṇṇīr corintu varavēṟṟa māntariṉ kātal kaṇṭu karainturukātār evarumillai. periyār pēḻaiyaip pāḷaiyaṅ kōṭṭait tirukkōvilukku eṭuttuc ceṉṟu cāṉṟōr maṟaimoḻi naviṉṟār; maṟṟaiyōr ēṅkit toḻutu niṉṟār. aṉṟiravu muḻutum annakark kirustavarkaḷ avviṭam viṭṭup peyara maṉamaṟṟu aḻuta vaṇṇamum, toḻuta vaṇṇamumāy amarntiruntārkaḷ. maṟunāṭ kālaiyil pēḻaiyai vaṇṭiyilēṟṟi iṭaiyaṉkuṭiyai nōkkip puṟappaṭṭārkaḷ. aiyariṟantāreṉṉum ceyti pāḷaiyaṅkōṭṭaiyiṉ vaḻiyāka ellā ūrkaḷilum paraviṟṟu. āṅkāṅku āyirakkaṇakkāṉa makkaḷ cālai yiṉirumaruṅkum vaḻimēl viḻivaittuk kāttu niṉṟār. pāḷaiyaṅ kōṭṭaiyiṉ ellai kaṭantapoḻutu pātai yiṉiru maruṅkum avalamuṟṟu niṉṟa iḷanaṅkaiyar aruṅ kaṇṇīr vaṭittu,
“nantā viḷakkaṉaiya nāyakaṉē nāṉilattōr
 tantāy taṉiaṟattiṉ tāyē tayānilaiyē”
 entāy iṟantaṉaiyē! emaikkāppār yāruḷarē!
eṉṟu parintu pāṭiya poḻutu karuṅkal maṉamum karainturukuvatāyiṟṟu.
iṅṅaṉam peruntuyariṉiṭaiyē naṭantu ceṉṟa vaṇṭi maṟunāṭkālai āṟu maṇikku iṭaiyaṉkuṭiyiṉ ellai vantuṟṟatu. aṅku tantaiyai iḻantavar pōla iravu muṟṟum tayaṅki niṉṟa makkaḷ pēḻaiyaik kaṇṭa pōḻtu viḻuntu aḻutu puraṇṭu araṟṟiṉār. ālayattiṉarukē iṟakki vaitta peṭṭiyiṉmītu avvūr makammatiya aṉparkaḷ veṟṟilaiyum viraimalarum kaiyuṟaiyāka vaittuk kaṇṇīrukuttār. appāl aiyar pēḻaiyai ālayattiṉuḷḷē uriya muṟaiyiṟ koṇṭu cella eṭuttārkaḷ. marukarāya viyattarum, māṇpu vāynta mārkkōciyarum, keḻutakaimai vāynta kemparum, vaṇṭamiḻaṟinta vākkarum, ēṉaiya āṅkilat toṇṭarum aṇiyaṇiyākap pēḻaiyiṉ muṉṉē ceṉṟār. aiyariṉ maintar aruñcōkamuṟṟup piṉṉē pōntār. maṟaimoḻiyāl muṟaiyāka iṟaivaṉait toḻutu pēḻaiyuḷ amainta perumai cāṉṟa tiruvuruvai aṉpārnta marukam ālayak kallaṟaiviṭṭār. arai nūṟṟāṇṭu iṭaiyaṉkuṭiyil aṟappaṇiyāṟṟi aḷaviṟanta nalam purintu,
‘aṉpilā rellām tamakkuriyar aṉpuṭaiyār
 eṉpum uriyar piṟarkku’
eṉṉum maṟaip poruḷai viḷakkiyaruḷiya kālṭuvel aiyariṉ takai cāṉṟa vāḻkkai naṉṟi maṟavāta naṟṟamiḻ makkaḷ uḷḷattai aḷḷuvatākum.
15. muṭivurai
aiyar iṟantamaiyāl aruṅkalaiyulakam ciṟanta aṟiñarai iḻantatu. tamiḻ nāṭu takaicāṉṟa maintaṉai iḻantatu. vēta viḷakkac caṅkam viḻumiya toṇṭarai iḻantatu. kāṟṟin taṭaṅkiya naṟṟavap periyāratu pirivaṟintu parivu kūrntār palar. kālṭuvel aiyariṉ paṇittiṟattiṉai viyantu āṅkila nāṭṭu mutalamaiccar1 maṉṉar tayāḷa nitiyiṉiṉṟu2 nūṟṟaimpatu pavuṉ avar maṉaiviyārkku aṉpu kūrntu vaḻaṅkiṉār. pala tuṟaikaḷilum aiyar āṟṟiya arumpaṇiyaip pārāṭṭi ceṉṉai aracāṅkamum palkalaik kaḻakamum parivut tīrmāṉaṅkaḷaip pāṅkuṟa niṟaivēṟṟiṉa.
aimpatāṇṭukaṭku mēlākak kaimmāṟu karutātu aṟappaṇiyāṟṟiya kālṭuvel aiyariṉ orumaiyai aḷantaṟital aritākum. āyiṉum avar vāḻkkaiyiṉ viḻumiya kuṟikkōḷ iṉitu viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. iḷam paruvattil kaviṉ kalaik kallūriyiṟ kālṭuvel aiyar payilum poḻutu taḷḷariya karuṇai veḷḷam avaruḷḷat taṭattiṟ poṅki eḻuntatu. maṉṉuyirkkākat taṉṉuyir vaḻaṅkiya tayāparaṉai niṉaintu ūṉ karaintu uyir karaintu urukiṉār. iṟaivaṉaip pōṟṟātu āṟṟa nāḷ pōkkiya puṉmaiyai niṉaintu maṉaṅ kuṟukiṉār. irupatām vayatil laṇṭaṉ mānakart toṇṭar caṅkattaic cārntār āvituṟakkum nilaiyilirunta aṉpārnta aṉṉaiyaik karttar kāvalil viṭṭut tamiḻnāṭṭai nōkkip puṟappaṭṭār. ceṉṉait tuṟaimukam cērumaḷavum ēcunātar perumaiyai pēci makiḻntār. ceṉṉai mānakaril mūṉṟāṇṭu camayat toṇṭarōṭu iṇaṅki iruntār. appāl poruṉai nāṭṭil nāṭōṟum nātaṉ paṇiyē nayantu purintār. kirustu matak koḷkaikaḷai ‘viṉā viṭai’yiṉ vāyilāka viḷakkip pōntār. iṉṉum  ‘ñāṉālayam’, ‘tiyāṉamālai’ mutaliya tamiḻ nūlkaḷ iyaṟṟiyaruḷiṉār. vētanūlaiyum pirārttaṉai nūlaiyum tamiḻil moḻipeyarkkum perum paṇiyil īṭupaṭṭirunta patiṉōrāṇṭum tam vāḻkkaiyiṉ paṇpārnta pākam eṉṟeṇṇi eṇṇi uḷḷam taḻaittār. curuṅkac colliṉ, karttarāl āṭkoḷḷappaṭṭa aṉṟē kālṭuvel aiyar tam uṭal, poruḷ, āviyeṉṉum mūṉṟaiyum avar paṇiyil uyttār eṉpatil eṭṭuṇaiyum aiyamillai. aiyar purinta arumpaṇiyiṉ payaṉāka iṭaiyaṉkuṭi vaṭṭattil ēcuviṉ pukaḻē niṟaintu niṉṟatu. poruṉai nāṭṭil aiyar arumpaṇi toṭaṅkum poḻutu nālāyiram kirustavar kāṇappaṭṭaṉar. avar vāḻkkai muṭivuṟum poḻutu nāṟpatiṉāyiram makkaḷ kirustu matattai mēṟkoṇṭu viḷaṅkiṉar. aṉṟiyum teṉṉāṭṭil muṉpu palarum paḻitturaittaṟku nilaikkaḷaṉāka niṉṟa kirustu matam aiyar arumpaṇiyāṟ paṇpuṟṟu nallōr pukaḻuraik kuriyatāyiṟṟu. kācukkum kañcikkum vakaiyaṟṟār kirustu matattai ēṟpār eṉṟu pāmara makkaḷ kūṟiya vacaivēraṟṟu vīḻntatu. ‘cōṟṟuk kirustavar’ eṉṟu aracāḷum vakuppiṉarāṟ karutappaṭṭa makkaḷ ippoḻutu kalviyaṟiviṟ ciṟantu viḷaṅkukiṉṟārkaḷ.3
poruṉai nāṭṭil aiyar ārvattāl kirustu matam viraintu paravi nilaipeṟak kaṇṭa āṅkila nāṭṭuk kirustu camaya caṅkattār avar cāṉṟāṇmaiyai viyantu pōṟṟiṉār. ṭarkām eṉṉum nakaril amainta carvakalā caṅkattār avar camayap pulamaiyai matittu ‘ṭākṭar’ eṉṉum kauravap paṭṭam vaḻaṅkiṉar. iṅṅaṉam mācilā maṉanalam vāynta camayat toṇṭarkaḷ palapaṭap pārāṭṭiyapoḻutu kālṭuvel aiyar tām āṟṟiya paṇiyiṉ ciṟumaiyaiyum, āṟṟātoḻinta paṇiyiṉ aḷaviṉaiyum niṉaintu akaṅkuṟukiṉār. mācaṟṟa ēcunātar kāṭṭiya neṟiyē mānila meṅkum paravit tikaḻavēṇṭumeṉa ārvamuṟṟa aiyar maṉattil tām ceytu muṭitta paṇiyiṉ curukkamum, piṉṉum ceyaṟpālatāya paṇiyiṉ perukkamum viḷaṅkit tōṉṟiṉa. ākavē carvakalā caṅkattār vaḻaṅkiya paṭṭam aiyar piṉṉum tūya naṟṟoṇṭu ceyvataṟkut tūṇṭukōlākavē amaintatu. teṉṉāṭṭil toṇṭu purinta vētaviḷakkac caṅkattiṉ varalāṟṟait tokuttu nūlākki veḷiyiṭṭār. ‘yātum ūrē yāvarum kēḷir’ eṉṉum paranta koḷkaiyaik kaṭaippiṭittu tamiḻakattait tāyakamākak koṇṭu, kaṟṟaṟintaṭaṅkiya naṟṟavac celvarāy, tammaiyē tamarkku nalkiya taṉipperuntoṇṭarāy viḷaṅkiya takkārai maṉakkōyiluḷ amaittup pōṟṟutal tamiḻ nāṭṭu makkaḷ kaṭaṉākum.4

aṭikkuṟippu
1.	Gladstone.
2.	The Royal Bounty Fund.
3.	“In 1838 (Caldwell) found the native converts sneered at by the governing race as ‘rice christians’ and disdained by the Brahmins and educated Hindu as a new low caste be gotten of ignorance and hunger. Not long before his death, the Director of public Instruction in Madras declared that if the native christians maintain the present rate of educational progress they will before long engross the leading positions in professional life in Southern India.” w.w.Hunter. - London Times, 19th October 1891.
4.	“A man of wide and varied Knowledge, a scholar of European reputation ful of philosophic and practical wisdom, yet he was content to live for half a century in a remote part of Tinnevelly. Spending and being spent for the benefit of the people of that province. The Tinnevelly native church owes him a debt of gratitude it can never repay.” - Rev. Wyatt, C.R. 187.
APPENDIX I
Dr. Caldwell’s life and work.
“By the death of the Right Reverrend Dr. Caldwell, Bishop of Tinnevelly, Christianity in Tinnevelly loses one of its most venerated fathers, and Indian scholarship one of its great original workers. During more than half a century Dr. Caldwell has, in spite of his retiring nature held a foremost place among his countrymen in India, not only as a leader and moving spirit of the missionary Church, but as a scholar without an equal in his special field of learning.... For more than fifty years Dr. Caldwell has been identified with this great work of humanity. In his funeral sermon at Kodaikanal last month, it was stated that he had seen the Christian in Tinnevelly increase from 6000 to 1,00,000. It is however as the investigator of the South Indian family of languages that Bishop Caldwell was most widely known. His comparative grammar of the Dravidian group originally published in 1856 was a revelation to western philologers and it remains, in the form of a second edition in 1875, the standard authority on the subject without a rival or a successor. His comparative grammar of tthe Dravidian languages will ever stand  forth as one of the monumental works of the age. But in this, as in every branch of his untiring labours, he was inspired with the belief that he was doing true missionary service. The literary work to which he himsellf looked back with greatest satisfaction was the part which he took during eleven years in the revision of the Tamil Bible and when that long labour was ended in the revision of the Tamil Book of Common Prayer.”
- The London Times, 19th Oct. 1891.
“A true servant of God, a profound scholar, a man of persistent and whole hearted devotion to the one great purpose of his life, he strove to raise and benefit in the highest and fullest sense the whole mass of the people in whose midst he spent more than half a century of his noble life. Their bodily, mental, spiritual and social welfare were all alike the objects of his unceasing solicitude and effort. The large village of Idayangudi, with its magnificent Church, its regular streets, commodious and comfortable houses, hospitals and schools and many other appliances for the welfare and elevation of the people abundantly illustrates the many side character of his great life work. Few Missionaries who came out to India surpassed him in scholarship, in success in the work of winnings souls or in breadth and saintliness of character. he was familiar with eithteen languages and has left behind him as abiding monuments of his learning a comparative grammar of the Dravidian languages and a History of Tinnevelly besides other literay productions of less importance.”
- The Centenary History of the C.M.S. in Tinnevelly 1923.

to.pē.: 24339030, cel: 9444410654
miṉṉañcal: naṭayaeyaṇāyapayavēa@paayaṭai.uṭia

āyiram viḷakku, ceṉṉai - 600 006. 

nuḻaivurai
tamiḻiṉattiṟkut tam iṉa uṇarvaiyum, moḻiyuṇarvaiyum ūṭṭi vaḷarttavarkaḷ tantai periyār, pēraṟiñar aṇṇā, taṉittamiḻ iyakkattiṉ tantai maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ, moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇar, pāvēntar pāratitācaṉ, paṉmoḻip pulavar kā. appātturaiyār, pāvalarēṟu peruñcittiraṉār mutalāṉa perumakkaḷ palarāvar.
paṉmoḻip pulavar kā. appātturaiyār iravu pakal pārātu uḻaittu eḻutiya nūlkaḷ 120 kkum mēṟpaṭṭavai (avaṟṟuḷ āṅkila nūlkaḷ aintu). eṅkaḷ kaikaḷukkuk kiṭaitta nūlkaḷ 97. avaṟṟaip poruḷvaḻip pirittuk kāla varicaippaṭutti 48 tokutikaḷāka appātturaiyam eṉum talaippil tamiḻ ulakukku vaḻaṅkukiṟōm.
tamiḻiṉam taṉ nilaiyuṇarat tavaṟiya vēḷaiyil tamiḻiṉ tūymaiyaiyum, tamiḻiṉattiṉ paṇṭaip perumaiyaiyum kāppataṟkut  takka vaḻikāṭṭi amaippukaḷāka 1916il toṭaṅkappaṭṭavai taṉittamiḻ iyakkamum, tirāviṭar iyakkamum ākum. ivviru iyakkaṅkaḷiṉ paṅkaḷippāl tamiḻiṉam eḻuccipeṟṟatu. ivvuṇmaiyai it ittokuppukaḷaip payilvōr eḷitāy uṇara muṭiyum.
tamiḻmoḻi āyvālum, tamiḻaka varalāṟṟu āyvālum, moḻi peyarpput tiṟattālum paṉmoḻip pulamaiyālum tamiḻiṉ mēṉmaikkup perum paṅkāṟṟiyavar appātturaiyār. 20ām nūṟṟāṇṭut tamiḻ aṟiñarkaḷuḷ mutal varicaiyil vaittup pōṟṟappaṭupavar avar. avar eḻutiya nūlkaḷiṉ eṇṇikkaiyaikkuṟittu periyavar mukam māmaṇi avarkaḷum, pērāciriyar ku.ve. pālacuppiramaṇiyaṉ avarkaḷum tattam nūlkaḷil vaḻaṅkiya varicaiyaip piṉpaṟṟi appātturaiyam tokuppukaḷai veḷiyiṭukiṟōm.
tamiḻakam muḻuvatum alaintu, perumuyaṟciyāl tēṭiccēkarittavai inta 97 nūlkaḷ. eṅkaḷukkuk kiṭaikkāta nūlkaḷaip piṉṉiṇaippil cērttuḷḷōm. annūlkaḷ vaittiruppōr vaḻaṅkiṉāl  naṉṟiyuṭaṉ aṭutta patippil cērttu veḷiyiṭuvōm. it tokuppukaḷil aṭaṅkiyuḷḷa nūlkaḷai uruvākkit tamiḻar kaikaḷil tavaḻa viṭuvataṟkut tokuppāciriyar muṉaivar kalpaṉā cēkkiḻār avarkaḷum, yāṉum peṟṟa iṭarppāṭukaḷ mikuti. arumai makaḷ muṉaivar kalpaṉā cēkkiḻār tam tokuppuraiyil ittokuppukaḷ evvāṟu uruvākkappaṭṭaṉa eṉpatai virivāka viḷakkiyuḷḷār.
appātturaiyāriṉ aṟivuc celvaṅkaḷait tamiḻkūṟum nallulakukku vaḻaṅkiya patippakaṅkaḷil mutaṉmaiyāṉatu caivacittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakam. tamiḻ-tamiḻar mūlaṅkaḷait tamiḻakam tēṭippaṭippataṟku aṭittaḷamāka amainta patippakam caivacittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakam.
pāri nilaiyam, maṇivācakar patippakam, vaḷḷuvar paṇṇai, pūmpukār patippakam, vacantā patippakam, tamiḻmaṇ patippakam muliya pala patippakaṅkaḷ ipperuntamiḻ aṟiñariṉ nūlkaḷai veḷiyiṭṭut tamiḻukku vaḷamum valimaiyum cērttuḷḷaṉa. 
innūlkaḷiṉ tokuppāciriyar pērāciriyar muṉaivar kalpaṉā cēkkiḻār avarkaḷ, tamiḻāyvuk kaḷattil kuṟippiṭattakkavar. tamiḻāyvup parappiṟku valimaiyūṭṭa ivarukkup pallāṟṟāṉum utavivarupavar ivaruṭaiya arumaik kaṇavar maruttuvar cēkkiḻār avarkaḷ. tamiḻppatippulakam, ivviṇaiyariṉ tamiḻkkāppup pēruḻaippai eṉṟum niṉaivu kūrum.
tokuppu nūlkaḷiṉ uḷḷaṭakkam ceppamāka uruvāvataṟkut taṉakkuḷḷa uṭal nalivaiyum tāṅkik koṇṭu uḻaitta tamiḻmakaḷ 
kō. cittirāvukku naṉṟi. tokuppukaḷiṉ mukappu aṭṭaikaḷ pala vaṇṇa vaṭivamaippuṭaṉ varuvataṟku uḻaitta celvaṉ. pā. ari (hariṣ) uḻaippiṟku nalla etirkālam uṇṭu.  it tokuppukaḷ ellā nilai
yilum naṉṟāka varuvataṟku uḷḷum puṟamum uḻaittu utaviyavar 
tiru. irā. paramēcuvaraṉ. patippucciṟappiṟku utaviya tiru. taṉacēkaraṉ, 
tiru. ku. marutu, tiru. vi. matimāṟaṉ innālvarum naṉṟikkuriyōr.
ittokuppukaḷil iṭampeṟṟuḷḷa nūlkaḷ pala iṭaṅkaḷilum tēṭic cērttavai. kaṉṉimārā nūlakattil irunta nūlkaḷaip paṭiyeṭuttu utaviya `kaṉṉimārā’ nūlakap paṇiyāḷarkaḷukkum `civakurunātaṉ centamiḻ nūl nilaiyam’ (kumpakōṇam), tāḷāḷar pērā. muṉaivar irāma kurunātaṉ avarkaḷukkum neñcam niṟainta naṉṟi. ceṉṉai taramaṇiyil iyaṅki varum rōcā muttaiyā nūlakap paṇiyāḷarkaḷ  utavikku naṉṟi. 
nūlkaḷai meyppup pārttu utaviyavar perumpulavar ayyā paṉacai aruṇā avarkaḷ. muṉaivar aru. apirāmi taṉ āciriyap paṇikkiṭaiyilum cōrvuṟātu patippakam vantu it tokuppukaḷ veḷivaruvataṟku ellā nilaiyilum utaviyavar. mēlum ittokuppukaḷ naṉṟāka veḷivaruvataṟku utaviyavarkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ taṉip pakkattil pativu ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
taṉakkeṉa vāḻātu, tamiḻkkeṉa vāḻntu, pallāṇṭuk kālam uḻaitta  paṉmoḻip pulavar kā. appātturaiyāriṉ nūlkaḷai appātturaiyam eṉum talaippil tamiḻarkaḷiṉ kaikaḷil tavaḻa viṭuvatil perumakiḻcci aṭaikiṟōm.
“ākkattai eṉakkin nāṭṭār
  aḷittiṭṭa aṟivai yellām 
  tēkkieṉ tamiḻmēṉ maikkē
  celaviṭak kaṭamaip paṭṭēṉ.”
 - pāvēntar

kō. iḷavaḻakaṉ
tokuppurai
maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷāriṭam paṭṭai tīṭṭap peṟṟa
taṉmāṉat tamiḻaṟiñar!
irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭut tamiḻ aṟivāḷumaikaḷil perum pulamaiyāḷar paṉmoḻippulavar kā. appātturaiyār. inti moḻi kaṟpikkum āciriyarāyt toṭaṅkiyatu avariṉ vāḻkkai. piṉpu tamiḻ, tamiḻar, tamiḻnāṭu eṉṉum cintaṉaiyil īṭupaṭṭār; nuṭpamāṉa palvēṟu āyvu nūlkaḷai eḻutiyum piṟa moḻikaḷil iruntu (ilakkiyam, āyvu, aṟiñarkaḷiṉ cintaṉaikaḷ pōṉṟa nūlkaḷ) tamiḻil moḻi peyarttum tamiḻiḻulakukku vaḻaṅkiṉār. avar nūlkaḷ tamiḻ āyvup parappil perum nalviḷaivukaḷai ēṟpaṭuttiṉa.
“avar tamiḻiṉ mūlattaiyē ārāya muṉaintavar. tamiḻiṉattiṉ varalāṟṟait turuvi turuvi ārāyvataṉ mūlam tamiḻ iṉattiṟkum maṟṟa iṉattiṟkum iṭaiyē tōḻamaiyai ēṟpaṭutta naṟpaṇi ceytirukkiṟār”  pēraṟiñar aṇṇā paṉmoḻip pulavariṉ āyvut taṉmaiyai ippaṭi  eṭuttukkāṭṭukiṟār.
pāvalarēṟu peruñcittiraṉār, paṉmoḻip pulavaraiyum, pāvāṇaraiyum oppiṭṭuk kāṭṭuvatu maṉaṅkoḷattakkatu. tēvanēyappāvāṇaraiyum - kā. appātturaiyāraiyum kuṟippiṭum pōtu, “ivviruvarum irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭup pulamaikku iraṇṭu mērumalaikaḷ; maṟainta kumarik kaṇṭattu ōṭiyirunta paḵṟuḷiyāṟum kumariyāṟum pōṉṟavarkaḷ; kaḻakap pulavaruḷ paraṇarum kapilarum pōṉṟa perumakkaḷ; moḻiyaiyum iṉattaiyum tūkki niṟutta vanta nuṇṇaṟi vāḷarkaḷ. ivarkaḷ kālattu maṟṟa piṟa pulavarkaḷ viṇmīṉkaḷ eṉṟāl, ivarkaḷ iruvarum katiravaṉum nilavum pōṉṟa antaṇarkaḷ; centamiḻ aṟavōrkaḷ; toṇṭu tavam iyaṟṟiya tīntamiḻt tuṟavōrkaḷ. moḻippaṟṟum, iṉappaṟṟum, nāṭṭuppaṟṟum koṇṭa nal uravōrkaḷ.” tamiḻulakiṟku appātturaiyār āṟṟiya paṇiyiṉ iṉṟiyamaiyāmaiyaiyum avaruṭaiya eḻuttukkaḷiṉ tēvaiyaiyum peruñcittiraṉār ivvāṟu uṇarttukiṟār.
camūkam palvēṟu kūṟukaḷai uḷḷaṭakkic ceyalpaṭṭuk koṇṭirukkum. acceyalpāṭukaḷ cariyāṉa vakaiyil amaintu uriya puḷḷiyil iṇaiyum poḻutu, ac camūkam mēleḻukiṟatu. pattoṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutikkālattil tamiḻakattil appaṭiyāṉa oṉṟaik kaṭṭiyamaikka vēṇṭiya nilai iruntatāl, ataṉ toṭaracciyāṉa ceyalpāṭukaḷum eḻuntaṉa. 
-	taṉittamiḻ iyakkat tōṟṟam
-	nītik kaṭci toṭakkam
-	nāṭṭu viṭutalai uṇarcci
-	tamiḻiṉa urimai eḻucci
-	pakuttaṟivu viḻippuṇarcci
-	intit tiṇippu etirppup pōr
-	putiya kalvi muṟaip payiṟci
-	putuvakai ilakkiya vaṭivaṅkaḷiṉ aṟimukam
ippaṭip palvēṟu taḷaṅkaḷil tamiḻakam taṉṉai maṟu kaṭṭamaippuc ceyya muṉaintukoṇṭiruntatu. ataṟkāṉa ottuḻaippum ceyaṟpāṭukaḷum palvēṟu nilaikaḷil tuṇaiyāka amaintaṉa.  appātturaiyāriṭam inti āciriyar - intit tiṇippu etirppu, pakti cārpu  -  pakuttaṟivuc cintaṉai, kāṅkiracuk koḷkai - tirāviṭak koḷkai, marapilakkiyam - navīṉa ilakkiyam eṉṉum muraṇnilaikaḷ iruntālum, “tamiḻ, tamiḻar, tamiḻnāṭu” eṉṉum taḷattil uṟutiyākac ceyalpaṭṭār. maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ, tēvanēyappāvāṇariṉ cintaṉaikaḷai uṭceṟittu, valuvāṉa karuttākkaṅkaḷai uruvākkiṉār. avai tamiḻiṉattiṉ maṟumalarccikku ūṉṟukōlāy amaintaṉa. 
“tāymoḻiyum, tāymoḻi ilakkiyamum, tāymoḻik kalviyumē maṉita nākarikattiṉ aṭippaṭai eṉpatu uṇarappaṭāmal intiyā neṭunāḷ vāḻa muṭiyātu. tamiḻar ivvuṇmaiyai aṟintu tamiḻaṟivum ulaka aṟivum oruṅkē peṟa utavum eṇṇam koṇṭē tamiḻilakkiya varalāṟṟilē ārvam ēṟpaṭāta ikkālattil ulaka varalāṟu eḻuta muṟpaṭṭōm” eṉpatu avar kūṟṟu, iṉṟum annilai muḻutāy uṇarappaṭāmal uḷḷatai eṉṉa colvatu!
appātturaiyār toṭakkattil intiya tēciyap pēriyakkattukkuḷ tammai iṇaittuk koṇṭu, kāntiyaṭikaḷiṉ koḷkaikaḷai ēṟṟār. ataṉaṭippaṭaiyil kānti rattiṉa tiruppukaḻ, kānti purāṇam, tāḻttap paṭṭōr kōyil nuḻaivu viḻā mutalāṉa pāṭalkaḷai itaḻkaḷil eḻutiṉār. kāṅkiracu muṉ vaitta moḻikkoḷkai kuṟippākat tamiḻakattil intiyait tiṇikka muṟpaṭṭa muyaṟci, tamiḻakattil perum kontaḷippai uruvākiyatu. inta nikaḻvu (1938 - 1939) avarait tamiḻar tēciyam nōkkit tiruppiyatu. ataṉāl periyāriṉ cuyamariyātai, pakuttaṟivuc cintaṉaikaḷōṭu tammai iṇaittukkoṇṭār. tamatu nilaippāṭṭai, avarē kūṟukiṟār. “car.ē. irāmacāmi mutaliyār pōṉṟavarkaḷ toṭakkattil cuyamariyātai iyakkattai ātarittup piṉ vilaka nērntatu. intak kālaṅkaḷil kāṅkiracai viṭṭō, caiva iyakkaṅkaḷai viṭṭō, tamiḻ iyakkaṅkaḷai viṭṭō vilakāmal niṉṟu, ellā muṟpōkku  vīrarkaḷaiyum iṇaikka nāṉ muyaṉṟēṉ. periyār itaṉai etirkkavillai. taṉmāṉa iyakkattiṟkum, tirāviṭa iyakkattukkum, tamiḻ iyakkattukkum eṉṉuṭaiya nilai iṉṟuvarai payaṉpaṭṭē vantuḷḷatu” - (aṟivuc curaṅkam, pak.100,101) paṉmoḻip pulavar oru kuṟippiṭṭa ellaikkuḷ (iyakkam cārntum eḻuttu cārntum) niṉṟuviṭāta urimaiyuṇarviṉar!
paṉmoḻip pulavar peyarāl veḷivanta mutal nūl kumarikkaṇṭam (1940-43). itu moḻipeyarppu nūl. kāḻi. kaṇṇucāmi piḷḷai cila pakkaṅkaḷ moḻipeyarttu iruntatai, muḻumaiyāka ivar moḻipeyarttuḷḷār. itaṉ toṭarcciyāka,
- 	ulaka ilakkiyaṅkaḷai, varalāṟukaḷait tamiḻil moḻi peyarttal.
- 	tamiḻ moḻi, iṉam toṭarpāṉa āṅkila nūlkaḷait tamiḻil tarutal.
 -	tamiḻ moḻi, iṉam, nāṭu cārnta cintaṉaiyākkaṅkaḷ vaḻaṅkal.
- 	tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷai ulaka ilakkiyaṅkaḷōṭu oppiṭṭu nōkki tamiḻ ilakkiyattiṉ ciṟappai uṇarttal.
- 	tirukkuṟaḷukku mika virivāṉa viḷakkavurai varaital.
- 	nuṇ viḷakkaṅkaḷuṭaṉ palvakai akarāti tokuttal.
inta aṭippaṭaiyil avaruṭaiya nūlkaḷ toṭarntu veḷivantuḷḷaṉa. 1947 - 1949 ām āṇṭukaḷil naṭuvaṇ araciṉ ceytit tuṟaiyil paṇiyāṟṟiya poḻutu, intiyāvil moḻic cikkal eṉṉum nūlai eḻutiṉār. innūlukku maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ 40 pakka aḷavil muṉṉurai vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷār. innūl eḻutiyataṉ kāraṇamāka avaratu aracup paṇi paṟikkappaṭṭatu.
 paṇiyiṉṟi irunta (1949 - 1959) kālakkaṭṭaṅkaḷil nūṟṟukku mēṟpaṭṭa nūlkaḷai eḻutiyuḷḷār. ivaruṭaiya nūlkaḷaic caivacittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakam, pārinilaiyam, āciriyar nūṟpatippuk kaḻakam, vaḷḷuvar paṇṇai, alamēlu patippakam pōṉṟa patippakaṅkaḷum piṟavum  veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷaṉa. tamiḻmaṇ patippakam ippōtu aṉaittu nūlkaḷaiyum 48 tokutikaḷāka veḷiyiṭum arumpaṇiyai niṟaivēṟṟiyuḷḷatu.
ulaka nākarikattiṉ vittu tamiḻ eṉat tam nuṇṇāyviṉ vaḻi niṟuviya, paṉmoḻip pulavariṉ aṉaittu nūlkaḷum tokukkappaṭa vēṇṭum eṉṟa vēṇavāviṉāl tamiḻmaṇ patippaka niṟuvaṉar aiyā iḷavaḻakaṉār it tokuppiṉai uruvākkap paṇittārkaḷ. aiyā avarkaḷ tamiḻukku āṟṟum pēruḻaippu eṉṉai viyakkac ceyyum. meyvaruttam pārāmal kaṇtuñcāmal eṭutta ceyalai nērttiyōṭu ceyya vēṇṭum eṉpatil uṟutiyāka niṟpavar. avaruṭaṉ iṇaintu ittokuppiṉai uruvākkuvatil makiḻcciyaṭaikiṟēṉ.
it tokuppiṟkāṉa nūlkaḷ kumpakōṇam centamiḻ nūlakam, rōcā muttaiyā nūlakam, kaṉṉimārā nūlakam, veṟṟiyaraci patippakam mutalāṉa  iṭaṅkaḷil iruntu tiraṭṭappeṟṟaṉa. pērāciriyar muṉaivar ku.ve. pāla cuppiramaṇiyam avarkaḷiṭamiruntum cila nūlkaḷ peṟappaṭṭaṉa. kiṭaitta nūlkaḷ 97. avai 48 tokutikaḷākat tokukkappaṭṭu veḷi varukiṉṟaṉa. at tokutikaḷ kīḻkkāṇum muṟaikaḷil pakukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
1.	tamiḻ - tamiḻar - tamiḻnāṭu
2.	varalāṟu
3.	āyvukaḷ
4.	moḻipeyarppu
5.	iḷaiyōr kataikaḷ
6.	potu nilai
perumpāṉmai nūlkaḷ ittokuppiṟkuḷ ataṉataṉ poruḷ aṭippaṭaiyilēyē tokukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. pakkac camanilai karuti māṟṟam peṟṟum uḷḷaṉa. vevvēṟu patippakaṅkaḷ orē nūlai vēṟu vēṟu peyarkaḷil veḷiyiṭṭiruntaṉa. cila nūlkaḷiṉ mutal patippu kiṭaikkāta nilai! kiṭaitta patippukaḷiṉ aṭippaṭaiyilēyē nūlkaḷ tokukkap paṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. mukam māmaṇi avarkaḷiṉ aṟivuccuraṅkam appātturaiyār eṉṟa nūlaiyum, pērāciriyar muṉaivar ku.ve. pāla cuppiramaṇiyam avarkaḷ eḻutiyuḷḷa paṉmoḻip pulavar kā. appātturai eṉṟa nūlaiyum aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭu appātturaiyam tokukkap paṭṭuḷḷatu. intak kāla varicai aṭippaṭaiyilāṉa nūṟpaṭṭiyal ittokuppil iṇaikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. appātturaiyār kuṟittu veḷivantuḷḷa kaṭṭuraikaḷ, aṟiñarkaḷ karuttukkaḷ, avar kuṟitta pāṭalkaḷ tiraṭṭappaṭṭu it tokuppukaḷil cērkkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. viṭupaṭṭa nūlkaḷ kiṭaikkac ceytāl aṭutta patippil naṉṟiyuṭaṉ veḷiyiṭappeṟum. avariṉ tirukkuṟaḷ viḷakkavurai ittuṭaṉ iṇaikkavillai. kāraṇam pakkam mikuntu iruppatē. kuṟaikaḷ iruppiṉ, cuṭṭik kāṭṭavum. maṟupatippil avai tiruttikkoḷḷappaṭum.
ittokuppu uruvāvataṟku ellāvakaiyilum muṉṉiṉṟavar aiyā tiru kō. iḷavaḻakaṉār. pakuppu muṟaikaḷaic caripārttuk koṭuttavar aiyā mutumuṉaivar irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉār. nūlkaḷait taṭṭaccu ceytum, nūliṉ 
uḷ vaṭivamaippiṉaic ceppam ceytum tantavar tirumati. kō. cittirā, tokuppu aṉaittiṟkum ciṟappuṟa mēl aṭṭaikaḷai vaṭivamaittavar celvaṉ. pā. ari (hariṣ), tokuppup paṇiyil tuṇai ceytōr eṉ āyvu māṇavarkaḷ tirumati. pā. mālati, tiru. kā. pāpu, celvaṉ. cu. kōvintarācu, celvi. kā. kayalviḻi. eṉ paṇikaḷ aṉaittiṟkum eṉṟum tuṇainiṟpavar kaṇavar maruttuvar mu. cēkkiḻār. ivarkaḷ aṉaivarukkum eṉṟum eṉ naṉṟiyum aṉpum uriyaṉa. 
kalpaṉā cēkkiḻār

 
nūlāciriyar vivaram
paṉmoḻip pulavar kā. appātturaiyār
iyaṟpeyar		:	nalla civam
piṟappu		:	 24.06.1907 	iṟappu: 26.05.1989
peṟṟōr		:	kācinātap piḷḷai, muttu ilakkumi
piṟanta ūr		:	kaṉṉiyākumari māvaṭṭam, 						āralvāy moḻi (aṟai vāy moḻi)
uṭaṉ piṟantōr		:	taṅkai iruvar, tampiyar iruvar
maṉaiviyar		: 	tirumati. nācciyār, tirumati. alamēlu
vaḷarppu makaḷ		:	tirumati. mallikā
toṭakkak kalvi		:	āralvāy moḻi
paḷḷik kalvi		:	nākarkōvil
kallūrik kalvi		:	tiruvaṉantapuram
		:	iḷaṅkalai āṅkilam (āṉars), 					mutukalai tamiḻ, inti `vicārat’, el.ṭi.
kaṟṟa moḻikaḷ		:	40 (puḻakkattil - tamiḻ, āṅkilam, 					camacukirutam, malaiyāḷam, inti)
nūlkaḷ		:	120 (āṅkilam, 5)
itaḻpaṇi		:	tirāviṭaṉ, jasṭis, intiyā, pāratatēvi, 					ciṉimā ulakam, lōkōpakāri, 						tārus islām, kumaraṉ, teṉṟal, viṭutalai.
paṇi	:	-	1936-37 tirunelvēli nācarēt 				pakutiyil intip piraccāra capā āciriyar.
		-	1937-1939 nellai em.ṭi.ṭi. 				kallūri inti āciriyar.
		-	paḷḷi āciriyar, 						ceṭṭināṭṭil amarāvatiputtūr maṟṟum			kōṉāppaṭṭu.
		-	1947-1949 maiya araciṉ ceytit 				toṭarputuṟaiyil paṇi
		-	1959 - 1965 ceṉṉaip palkalaikkaḻaka 			āṅkilam tamiḻ akarātip paṇiyil 				iṇai āciriyar.
		-	1975-1979 tamiḻaka varalāṟṟuk kuḻu 				uṟuppiṉar
aṟiñar toṭarpu:
-	toṭakkattil kāntiya cintaṉai.
-	1938-39 il inti etirppu iyakkam, periyār, aṇṇā, pāratitācaṉ kavimaṇi tēciya vināyakam piḷḷai, maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ, pāvēntar, pāvalarēṟu, tēvanēyap pāvāṇar  maṟṟum camakāla aṟiñar perumakkaḷ, paṭaip pāḷumaikaḷ toṭarpu
virutukaḷ: 	
-	maturaiyil  nikaḻnta 5āvatu ulakat tamiḻ mānāṭṭil poṟkiḻiyum kēṭayamum vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu, 
- 	1973 il centamiḻc celvar, cēlam tamiḻakap pulavar kuḻu kūṭṭattil `cāṉṟōr paṭṭam’, `tamiḻaṉpar’ paṭṭam.
-	1981 caṉavari 26 il tamiḻnāṭu iyal, icai, nāṭaka maṉṟam cārpil `kalaimāmaṇi’.
-	1983 il tamiḻnāṭu aracu vaḻaṅkiya `tiru.vi.ka.’ virutu, taṅkap patakkam.
-	maturaik kāmarācar palkalaik kaḻakam ciṟappittu vaḻaṅkiya `pēravaic cemmal’ virutu.
-	1961 il ceṉṉait tamiḻ eḻuttāḷar caṅkattiṉ talaivar.
-	1970 il pārīcil naṭaipeṟṟa ulakat tamiḻ mānāṭṭil ciṟappu uṟuppiṉarākak kalantu koṇṭār.
-	iṅkilāntu ākcupōṭu palkalaikkaḻakam ivaratu `teṉṉāṭṭup pōrkkaḷaṅkaḷ’ nūlai aṅkup paṭikkum mēlpaṭṭap paṭippu māṇavarkaḷukkup pāṭamāka vaikkappaṭṭu iruntatu.
paṉmoḻippulavariṉ vāḻkkai varalāṟṟu nūlkaḷ:
-	aṟivuc curaṅkam kā. appātturaiyār, mukamāmaṇi, māṇavar patippakam, ceṉṉai -17, 2005.
-	paṉmoḻip pulavar kā. appātturaiyār,		pērā.muṉaivar. ku.ve. pālacuppiramaṇiyam, cākittiya akātemi, 2007.

 
patippāḷar vivaram
kō. iḷavaḻakaṉ 
piṟanta nāḷ		:	3.7.1948
piṟanta ūr		:	uṟantairāyaṉkuṭikkāṭu añcal						urattanāṭu vaṭṭam - 614 625						tañcāvūr māvaṭṭam.
kalvi		:	kallūri pukumuka vakuppu
ippōtaiya toḻil	:	puḻakkattil illāta paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷait 				tēṭiyeṭuttu veḷiyiṭal
āṟṟiyuḷḷa potuppaṇikaḷ
1965il paḷḷi māṇavaṉāka iruntapōtē moḻip pōrāṭṭattil muṉaippākap paṅkēṟṟut taḷaippaṭuttap peṟṟu 48 nāḷkaḷ ciṟaiyil iruntavar.
piṟanta ūrākiya uṟantairāyaṉkuṭikkāṭṭil `ūrnalaṉ vaḷarccik kaḻakam’ eṉum camūka amaippil iruntu ūr nalappaṇi āṟṟiyavar.
urattanāṭṭil `tamiḻar urimaik kaḻakam’ eṉṉum amaippaiyum, pāvāṇar paṭippakattaiyum naṇparkaḷuṭaṉ iṇaintu niṟuvit tamiḻmoḻi, tamiḻiṉa, tamiḻaka mēmpāṭṭiṟku uḻaittavar. iḷam talaimuṟaikkut tamiḻt toṇṭāṟṟiyavar.
pēraṟiñar aṇṇāviṉ matuvilakkuk koḷkaiyai neñcil ēnti urattanāṭu matuvilakkuk kuḻuviṉ mukkiya amaippāḷarkaḷil oruvarāka iruntu ceyalāṟṟiyavar. 1975-il tamiḻnāṭu caṭṭamaṉṟattil `urattanāṭu tiṭṭam’ eṉṟu pārāṭṭap peṟṟa matuvilakkut tiṭṭam veṟṟi peṟa uḻaittavar.
tamiḻmaṇ patippakattai niṟuvi puḻakkattil illāta  paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷaiyum, putiya paṭaippu ilakkiyaṅkaḷaiyum, 
19  ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyilum 20ām nūṟṟāṇṭut toṭakkat tilum vāḻnta tamiḻccāṉṟōrkaḷ eḻuti vaittuc ceṉṟa tamiḻiṉ aṟivuc celvaṅkaḷait tēṭi eṭuttu muḻumaiyākap poruḷ vaḻip pirittu, kāla niralil toṭar toṭarāka  veḷiyiṭṭut tamiḻnūl patippil taṉi muttirai patittu varupavar.
potunilai
taṉittamiḻ iyakkat tantai maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ, tantai periyār, pāvēntar pāratitācaṉ, pēraṟiñar aṇṇā,  moḻinūl mūtaṟiñar ñā. tēvanēyap pāvāṇar,  pāvalarēṟu peruñcittiraṉār ākiyōrai vaḻikāṭṭikaḷākak koṇṭu avartam koḷkai kaḷai niṟaivēṟṟa ayarātu uḻaittu varupavar.
 
tokuppāciriyar vivaram
muṉaivar kalpaṉā cēkkiḻār
piṟanta nāḷ		:	5.6.1972
piṟanta ūr		:	okkanāṭu kīḻaiyūr							urattanāṭu vaṭṭam - 614 625						tañcāvūr māvaṭṭam.
kalvi		:	mutukalai (tamiḻ, moḻiyiyal, kaṇiṉiyiyal) 				muṉaivar
ippōtaiya paṇi	:	utavip pērāciriyar, tamiḻiyiyal tuṟai,				aṇṇāmalaip palkalaikkaḻakam.
āṟṟiyuḷḷa kalvippaṇikaḷ
-	aṇṇāmalaip palkalaikkaḻakat tamiḻiyal tuṟaiyil 12 āṇṭukaḷ utavip pērāciriyar paṇi.
-	tirukkuṟaḷ paritiyār uraip patippu, pariti urai āyvu.
- 	puṟanāṉūṟṟil tamiḻar vāḻviyal, aiṅkuṟunūṟṟu urupaṉiyal pakuppāyvu, caṅka ilakkiyap peṇpāṟ pulavarkaḷ pāṭal kaḷ moḻinaṭai - matippīṭu (tokuppu), caṅka ilakkiya ūrppeyar āyvukaḷ ākiya nūlkaḷiṉ āciriyar.
- 	palkalaikkaḻaka māṉiyakkuḻu, cemmoḻit tamiḻāyvu mattiya niṟuvaṉattiṉ mūlam āyvuttiṭṭaṅkaḷ peṟṟu āyvukaḷai nikaḻttiyuḷḷār.
-	palkalaikkaḻaka māṉiyakkuḻuviṉāl vaḻaṅkappaṭṭuḷḷa mēlāyviṉai (ñaṉuku) mēṟkoṇṭu varukiṟār.
-	50kkum mēṟpaṭṭa āyvuk kaṭṭuraikaḷai eḻutiyuḷḷār.
-	malēciyāvil nikaḻnta taṉittamiḻ iyakka nūṟṟāṇṭu viḻāvil ciṟappu aḻaippāḷarāka aḻaikkappaṭṭār.
-	ilaṅkaiyil naṭaipeṟṟa urainaṭai mānāṭṭil kalantu koṇṭu kaṭṭurai vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷār.
- 	cemmoḻit tamiḻāyvu mattiya niṟuvaṉattāl vaḻaṅkap paṭṭa kuṭiyaracu talaivariṉ iḷam aṟiñar virutiṉaip peṟṟuḷḷār.
